Refined Umber is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] item obtained from refining in [[The Forge]] in the [[Dwarven Mines]].
## Obtaining
Refined Umber can be forged once the player has reached [[Umber|Umber III (3)]] and [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain VII (7)]]. It requires 160 (2 stacks plus 32) [[Enchanted Umber]] and 1 hour to forge.
### Forging Tree
- [[Refined Umber]] 1 Refined Umber
  - 160 [[Enchanted Umber]] 25,600 (400 stacks) [[Umber]]
### Frozen Corpses
Refined Umber can be obtained from opening [[Frozen Corpses]].
|Type|Quantity|Chance Per Roll|
|-|-|-|
|Lapis|[[Refined Umber]] 1 Refined Umber|0.15% (1 in 670)|
|Umber Tungsten|[[Refined Umber]] 1 Refined Umber|0.31% (1 in 327)|
|Vanguard|[[Refined Umber]] 1 Refined Umber|4.55% (1 in 21.97)|
|Vanguard|[[Refined Umber]] 2 Refined Umber|2.28% (1 in 43.94)|

The Reindeer Backpack Skin is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Backpack Skins|Backpack Skin]] that could be purchased from a [[Fire Sale]].
## Obtaining
The Reindeer Backpack Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on December 24, 2022. Being [[Rarity|EPIC]], it cost 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 5,000 purchases.
## Usage
The Reindeer Backpack Skin can be applied to a [[Backpack|Backpacks]] by combining it in an [[Anvil]] to change its appearance.

## Skins
|Slot|Name|Rarity|Obtaining|Notes & Versions|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Smoldering Ember Skin]]|[[Smoldering Ember Skin]]|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|Seasonal Bundles ([[Taylor]]) - Summer 2024 - 1,350 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|This skin is animated!|
|[[Smoldering Ember Skin]]|[[Smoldering Ember Skin]]|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|(same as above)||
## Trivia
- This [[Armor]] set can be paired with a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Magma Cube Pet]] to buff the Armor's [[Stats]] by up to 50% at Level 100. (The Armor [[Item Upgrades|Upgrades]] will be buffed too).
- This armor is a direct reference to the [[Ember Armor]], a legacy armor set crafted with materials from the old [[Magma Cube Boss]] in the [[Blazing Fortress]].
## Tips
- Currently, the tiered bonus is bugged to not do anything. The following advice is written for if the bonus is fixed to work properly.
- This armor is a viable option for early to mid game, due to it only costing for decent stats as well as [[Gemstone Slot|Gemstone Slots]]. The main part of the armor, however, is the extra [[Fire Aspect]] damage. Using [[Fire Aspect|Fire Aspect III]], the 600% outgoing burning damage results in an effective 1.495x multiplier to first hit damage (1.661x at 800%), allowing the user to kill [[Ghost|Ghosts]] or [[Sven Packmaster|Sven Packmasters]] in 1 hit.
  - Furthermore, due to being a tiered armor set, it can be paired nicely with the [[Tarantula Armor|Tarantula Helmet]] or [[Primordial Armor|Primordial Helmet]].
  - Due to the armor stats not mattering as much as the passive, it is recommended to avoid using the [[Magma Cube Pet]], focusing instead on pets increasing damage such as the [[Lion Pet]] or [[Wither Skeleton Pet]].
  - While this armor is better than base [[Crimson Armor]] at first, it gets outclassed at Fiery Crimson or higher.

Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 84x (1 stack plus 20 (84 in total)) [[Freshly-Minted Coins]] 20x [[Emerald]] 5x [[Gold Ingot]]
Raw Materials to upgrade: 64x [[Freshly-Minted Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: RELIC_OF_COINS
Tooltip: Accessory Power: +12; A lot of coins start appearing around you occasionally on public islands. You may even pick up a Stock of Stonks!; Works while in Accessory Bag!; EPIC ACCESSORY
The Relic of Coins is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Accessories|Accessory]]. It is the final upgrade of the [[Talisman of Coins]] line and spawns the most [[Coins]] of the four.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[Artifact of Coins]] 64x [[Freshly-Minted Coins]]|Recipe: [[Freshly-Minted Coins]] x16 ×4 + [[Artifact of Coins]] → Relic of Coins|
## Usage
The Relic of Coins occasionally spawns groups of [[Coins]] on the ground around the player while on public islands. Any player on the server can see and collect these coins. Picking up a group awards the player [[Coins]].
Like the lower tiers, the effect does not function on the [[Private Island]] or in [[The Garden]].
Every minute, the Relic of Coins has a 0.002472% (1 in 40,444 minutes) chance of dropping a [[Stock of Stonks]], which averages out to roughly one every 674 hours.

# Remnant of the Eye
Aliases: Remnant Of The Eye
Categories: Epic items, Items, The End, Ender
[Remnant of the Eye]
Type: Item
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
Uses: Keeps you alive when you're on death's door, granting a short period of invincibility.; Consumed on use.; NOTE: ONLY WORKS ON THE END ISLAND.
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Merchants: [[Pearl Dealer]]
Buy: 200,000 [[Coins]]
Sell: 1 Coin ([[Coins]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: REMNANT_OF_THE_EYE
Tooltip: Keeps you alive when you are on death's door, granting a short period of invincibility. Consumed on use.; ONLY WORKS ON THE END ISLAND.; EPIC
The Remnant of the Eye is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] item. It prevents death within [[The End]].
## Obtaining
When an [[Ender Dragon]] spawns, all [[Sleeping Eye|Sleeping Eyes]] within all players' inventories will be converted into Remnants of the Eye.
Remnants of the Eye can also be purchased from the [[Pearl Dealer]] for 200,000 [[Coins]].
## Usage
If the player would die in [[The End]] while carrying a Remnant of the Eye in their inventory, they are instead healed to full health and granted a short period of invincibility, and the item is consumed. If the death would have been caused by the Void, the player is instead returned to spawn without losing any coins.
It does not work within the [[Void Sepulture]]. If the player dies there while carrying Remnants of the Eye, they die normally, but their Remnants of the Eye are not consumed.
## Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|The player would die outside [[Void Sepulture]].|Your Remnant of the Eye saved you from certain death!|
|The player falls into the Void.|Your Remnant of the Eye saved you from certain death! You were safely teleported back to spawn.|
|The player would die in [[Void Sepulture]].|Your Remnant of the Eye isn't strong enough for the magic of the Sepulture!|

Revenant Armor is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Armor]] Set unlocked at [[Zombie Slayer|Zombie Slayer IV (4)]]. It focuses on healing and [[Defense]] against [[Zombie|Zombies]].
Although crafting a Revenant Chestplate requires [[Zombie Slayer|Zombie Slayer V (5)]], only [[Zombie Slayer|Zombie Slayer IV (4)]] is needed to use it. It is an [[Undead Items|Undead]] armor set and therefore affected by the [[Zombie Pet]]'s Living Dead perk.

## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Revenant Chestplate ([[Revenant Armor]])|Zombie Slayer IV (4) ([[Zombie Slayer]])|4x [[Revenant Viscera]] 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Enchanted Diamond]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Diamond]] x32 ×4 + [[Revenant Viscera]] ×4 → [[Revenant Armor]]|
|Revenant Leggings ([[Revenant Armor]])|Zombie Slayer IV (4) ([[Zombie Slayer]])|3x [[Revenant Viscera]] 64x [[Enchanted Diamond]]|Recipe: [[Revenant Viscera]] ×3 + [[Enchanted Diamond]] x16 ×4 → [[Revenant Armor]]|
|Revenant Boots ([[Revenant Armor]])|Zombie Slayer IV (4) ([[Zombie Slayer]])|2x [[Revenant Viscera]] 64x [[Enchanted Diamond]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Diamond]] x32 ×2 + [[Revenant Viscera]] ×2 → [[Revenant Armor]]|
## Usage
Armor Set:
|Chestplate|Leggings|
|-|-|
|Revenant Chestplate ([[Revenant Armor]]); [[Health]]: +180 [[Defense]]: +70 Piece Bonus: Zombie Bulwark; Kill Zombies ([[Zombie]]) to accumulate [[Defense]] against them.|Revenant Leggings ([[Revenant Armor]]); [[Health]]: +130 [[Defense]]: +50 Piece Bonus: Zombie Bulwark; Kill Zombies ([[Zombie]]) to accumulate [[Defense]] against them.|
|Boots||
|Revenant Boots ([[Revenant Armor]]); [[Health]]: +100 [[Defense]]: +30 Piece Bonus: Zombie Bulwark; Kill Zombies ([[Zombie]]) to accumulate [[Defense]] against them.||
[Total]
[Revenant Armor Health: +400 Defense: +150]
[Effects]
[Full Set Bonus: Trolling The Reaper; Healing Wands are +50% more effective. Gain +100 Defense against Zombies (Zombie).]
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Reaper Chestplate ([[Reaper Armor]])|Zombie Slayer VII (7) ([[Zombie Slayer]])|1x Revenant Chestplate ([[Revenant Armor]]) 1x [[Revenant Catalyst]] 6x [[Enchanted Diamond Block]] 64x [[Revenant Viscera]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Diamond Block]] ×6 + [[Revenant Catalyst]] + [[Revenant Armor]] + [[Revenant Viscera]] x64 → [[Reaper Armor]]|
|Reaper Leggings ([[Reaper Armor]])|Zombie Slayer VII (7) ([[Zombie Slayer]])|1x Revenant Leggings ([[Revenant Armor]]) 1x [[Revenant Catalyst]] 6x [[Enchanted Diamond Block]] 64x [[Revenant Viscera]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Diamond Block]] ×6 + [[Revenant Catalyst]] + [[Revenant Armor]] + [[Revenant Viscera]] x64 → [[Reaper Armor]]|
|Reaper Boots ([[Reaper Armor]])|Zombie Slayer VII (7) ([[Zombie Slayer]])|1x [[Revenant Catalyst]] 1x Revenant Boots ([[Revenant Armor]]) 6x [[Enchanted Diamond Block]] 64x [[Revenant Viscera]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Diamond Block]] ×6 + [[Revenant Catalyst]] + [[Revenant Armor]] + [[Revenant Viscera]] x64 → [[Reaper Armor]]|
The [[Wands|Healing Wand]] bonus requires the set to have been worn for at least 3 seconds to activate.
## Tips
- Farming low level [[Zombie|Zombies]] in the [[Graveyard]] is an easy way to increase the kill counter on Zombie Bulwark.
- Donating this item in the [[Museum]] grants both the [[Zombie Armor]] and [[Revenant Armor]] Revenant Armor credit, despite not using Zombie Armor in the crafting recipe.
## Trivia
- In version v0.7.7, the Revenant Boots recipe was bugged, requiring an additional 16 [[Revenant Viscera]] on the bottom-right of the crafting recipe.
- Revenant Leggings used to be chainmail before changing to diamond during the Reforge Update.

# Revenant Catalyst
Categories: Epic items, Items in Sacks, Items, Crafting ingredients, Slayer, Zombie Slayer, Mob Drops
[Revenant Catalyst]
Type: Item
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
Collection ([[Collections]]): Zombie Slayer VI (6) ([[Zombie Slayer]])
Drop Chance: 7Extraordinary ([[Drop Chance]])
Tooltip Text: Drops rarely from Revenant Horror when you are at least Zombie Slayer LVL 6.
Source: [[Revenant Horror]] Tier II+
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Small Slayer Sack ([[Slayer Sack]]); Medium Slayer Sack ([[Slayer Sack]]); Large Slayer Sack ([[Slayer Sack]])
[Shop]
Sell: 8,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: REVENANT_CATALYST
Tooltip: Drops rarely from Revenant Horror when you are at least Zombie Slayer LVL 6.; Catalysts upgrade other items!; Right-click to view upgrades!; EPIC
The Revenant Catalyst is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] item dropped by the [[Revenant Horror]] Tier II or higher after obtaining [[Zombie Slayer|Zombie Slayer VI (6)]]. The chances of it dropping are [[Drop Chance|7Extraordinary]]. This [[Catalyst (disambiguation)|Slayer Catalyst]] can be used to craft the [[Reaper Falchion]] and [[Reaper Armor]].
## Obtaining
### Zombie Slayer LVL 6
1 Revenant Catalyst can be claimed once as a reward from [[Zombie Slayer|Zombie Slayer LVL VI (6)]].
### Revenant Horror
A Tier II+ [[Revenant Horror]] has a [[Drop Chance|7Extraordinary]] to drop 1 Revenant Catalyst if the player has reached [[Zombie Slayer|Zombie Slayer LVL VI (6)]].
Drop Chances:
- Tier II: 1.01% (1/99)
- Tier III: 1.01% (1/99.48)
- Tier IV: 1.01% (1/99.616)
- Tier V: 0.86% (1/116.216)
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Reaper Falchion]]|Zombie Slayer VI (6) ([[Zombie Slayer]])|1x [[Revived Heart]] [[Revenant Catalyst]] 1x Revenant Catalyst 1x [[Revenant Falchion]]|Recipe: [[Revived Heart]] + Revenant Catalyst + [[Revenant Falchion]] → [[Reaper Falchion]]|
|Reaper Chestplate ([[Reaper Armor]])|Zombie Slayer VII (7) ([[Zombie Slayer]])|1x Revenant Chestplate ([[Revenant Armor]]) [[Revenant Catalyst]] 1x Revenant Catalyst 6x [[Enchanted Diamond Block]] 64x [[Revenant Viscera]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Diamond Block]] ×6 + Revenant Catalyst + [[Revenant Armor]] + [[Revenant Viscera]] x64 → [[Reaper Armor]]|
|Reaper Leggings ([[Reaper Armor]])|Zombie Slayer VII (7) ([[Zombie Slayer]])|1x Revenant Leggings ([[Revenant Armor]]) [[Revenant Catalyst]] 1x Revenant Catalyst 6x [[Enchanted Diamond Block]] 64x [[Revenant Viscera]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Diamond Block]] ×6 + Revenant Catalyst + [[Revenant Armor]] + [[Revenant Viscera]] x64 → [[Reaper Armor]]|
|Reaper Boots ([[Reaper Armor]])|Zombie Slayer VII (7) ([[Zombie Slayer]])|[[Revenant Catalyst]] 1x Revenant Catalyst 1x Revenant Boots ([[Revenant Armor]]) 6x [[Enchanted Diamond Block]] 64x [[Revenant Viscera]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Diamond Block]] ×6 + Revenant Catalyst + [[Revenant Armor]] + [[Revenant Viscera]] x64 → [[Reaper Armor]]|

The Revived Heart is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Helmets|Helmet]]. It is unlocked at [[Zombie Slayer|Zombie Slayer VI (6)]], is the upgraded form of the [[Crystallized Heart]], and is the final form of the [[Zombie's Heart]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Zombie Slayer VI (6) ([[Zombie Slayer]])|1x [[Crystallized Heart]] 8x [[Zombie's Heart]]|Recipe: [[Zombie's Heart]] ×8 + [[Crystallized Heart]] → Revived Heart|
## Usage
Helmet:
Revived Heart [[Health]]: +100 [[Defense]]: +35 [[Intelligence]]: +100 [[Vitality]]: +50 [[Mending]]: +50
Due to being an [[Undead Items|Undead]] armor piece, the Revived Heart is affected by the [[Zombie Pet]]'s Living Dead perk.
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Reaper Falchion]]|Zombie Slayer VI (6) ([[Zombie Slayer]])|[[Revived Heart]] 1x Revived Heart 1x [[Revenant Catalyst]] 1x [[Revenant Falchion]]|Recipe: Revived Heart + [[Revenant Catalyst]] + [[Revenant Falchion]] → [[Reaper Falchion]]|
|[[Reaper Mask]]|Zombie Slayer LVL VII (7) ([[Zombie Slayer]])|[[Revived Heart]] 1x Revived Heart 1x [[Beheaded Horror]] 32x [[Enchanted String]] 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Revenant Viscera]]|Recipe: [[Revenant Viscera]] x32 ×4 + [[Beheaded Horror]] + [[Enchanted String]] x32 + Revived Heart → [[Reaper Mask]]|
|[[Warden Helmet]]|Zombie Slayer VIII (8) ([[Zombie Slayer]])|1x [[Warden Heart]] [[Revived Heart]] 2x Revived Heart 128x [[Enchanted Iron Block]]|Recipe: Revived Heart ×2 + [[Warden Heart]] + [[Enchanted Iron Block]] x64 ×2 → [[Warden Helmet]]|

The Rift Ferret Pet is a [[Pets|Combat Pet]]. It is [[Soulbound]] and [[Rift Dimension|# Rift-Transferable #]].
## Obtaining
An [[Rarity|EPIC]] Rift Ferret Pet can be purchased from [[Kat (Rift)|Neuroscientist Kat]] for 12 [[Riftwart Roots]] and 3,000 [[Motes]] after having deposited 20 Flies, 10 Spiders and 20 Silverfish in the [[Kat (Rift)|Vermin Bin]] with a [[Turbomax Vacuum Cleaner]].

## Usage
Aside from being used as a pet, a Rift Ferret can be given to [[George]] for 50,000 [[Coins]], as well as increasing your [[Taming]] level cap by 1 (the latter can only be done once).

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: RITUAL_RESIDUE
Tooltip: It's all that's left from a draconic sacrifice.; Right-click to view recipes!; EPIC
Ritual Residue is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] item used for crafting. It has a 10.84% chance to be obtained from sacrificing a piece of [[Dragon Armor]] and a 10.59% from sacrificing an [[Aspect of the Dragons]] at the [[Draconic Altar]].
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Draconic Talisman]]|None|[[Ritual Residue]] 2x Ritual Residue 32x [[Absolute Ender Pearl]] 96x (1 stack plus 32 (96 in total)) [[Mite Gel]]|Recipe: Ritual Residue ×2 + [[Mite Gel]] x16 ×6 + [[Absolute Ender Pearl]] x32 → [[Draconic Talisman]]|
|[[Draconic Ring]]|None|1x [[Draconic Talisman]] 1x [[Tessellated Ender Pearl]] [[Ritual Residue]] 4x Ritual Residue 96x [[Mite Gel]]|Recipe: Ritual Residue ×4 + [[Mite Gel]] x32 ×3 + [[Draconic Talisman]] + [[Tessellated Ender Pearl]] → [[Draconic Ring]]|
|[[Draconic Artifact]]|None|1x [[Draconic Ring]] 2x [[Tessellated Ender Pearl]] [[Ritual Residue]] 12x Ritual Residue 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Mite Gel]]|Recipe: Ritual Residue x3 ×4 + [[Tessellated Ender Pearl]] ×2 + [[Mite Gel]] x64 ×2 + [[Draconic Ring]] → [[Draconic Artifact]]|
|[[Dragonfuse Glove]]|None|[[Ritual Residue]] 4x Ritual Residue 48x [[Absolute Ender Pearl]] 72x (1 stack plus 8 (72 in total)) [[Mite Gel]]|Recipe: Ritual Residue x2 ×2 + [[Mite Gel]] x24 ×3 + [[Absolute Ender Pearl]] x16 ×3 → [[Dragonfuse Glove]]|
|[[Deepterror Mixin]]|None|[[Ritual Residue]] 1x Ritual Residue 64x [[Mite Gel]]|Recipe: [[Mite Gel]] x16 ×4 + Ritual Residue → [[Deepterror Mixin]]|
|[[Dragonfade Cloak]]|None|[[Ritual Residue]] 3x Ritual Residue 32x [[Absolute Ender Pearl]] 64x [[Mite Gel]]|Recipe: Ritual Residue ×3 + [[Mite Gel]] x16 ×4 + [[Absolute Ender Pearl]] x16 ×2 → [[Dragonfade Cloak]]|

# Robur Glyph
Categories: Epic items, Items, Pages with UIs
[Robur Glyph]
Type: Item
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Rift Transferrable ([[Rift Dimension]]): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: GLYPH_ROBUR
Tooltip: A scroll ingrained with an ancient symbol.; If the pattern is mimicked with a powerful artifact, it may be possible to cast a Warding Spell.; EPIC
The Robur Glyph is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] Quest Item obtained in the [[Rift Dimension]].
## Obtaining
The Robur Glyph can be obtained after starting [[Sorcerer Okron]]'s quest and imbuing the [[Vis Glyph]], then opening the chest at [[Coordinates|-33.5, 87.5, -93.5]] on top of the [[Infested House]]'s chimney. Once given to Okron, it can no longer be obtained.
## Usage
The Robur Glyph can be given to [[Sorcerer Okron]]. When given, he will task the player with using the [[Warding Diamathame]] to imbue a spell onto the house.
## Game Messages
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|You found Robur Glyph! You should return it to Okron...|Finding the Glyph|
|You already have one in your inventory!|Trying to claim another Glyph while already owning one|

- The map image shown while holding the Robur Glyph

- The imbued particles surrounding the house

The Royal Skin is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Helmet Skins|Helmet Skin]] that could be obtained from a past [[Taylor|Seasonal Bundle]] from [[Taylor]].
## Obtaining
The Royal Skin could be purchased from the Fall 2024 [[Taylor|Seasonal Bundle]] from [[Taylor]]. Being [[Rarity|EPIC]], it cost 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]]. However, due to being before the Winter 2024 Bundle, it cannot appear in future bundles, making it now unobtainable.
## Usage
The Royal Skin can be applied to a [[Mineral Armor|Mineral Helmet]] or [[Glossy Mineral Armor|Glossy Mineral Helmet]] by combining it in an [[Anvil]] to change its appearance.

## Usage
### Reforging
Sadan's Brooch can be applied to [[Armor]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Empowered]] reforge, to grant [[Health]] and [[Defense]], which scale with the armor's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining XXV (25)]] and can be done with any armor, regardless of the piece. Armor that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Empowered]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.
Additionally, it grants the Empowered Bonus, which +10 [[Mending]] while in Dungeons.
|Stat Bonus|Rarity COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity RARE ([[Rarity]])|Rarity EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Rarity MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity DIVINE ([[Rarity]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Stat Bonus|+10 HP ([[Health]]) +10 Def ([[Defense]])|+15 HP ([[Health]]) +15 Def ([[Defense]])|+20 HP ([[Health]]) +20 Def ([[Defense]])|+25 HP ([[Health]]) +25 Def ([[Defense]])|+35 HP ([[Health]]) +35 Def ([[Defense]])|+50 HP ([[Health]]) +50 Def ([[Defense]])|+70 HP ([[Health]]) +70 Def ([[Defense]])|
|Reforge Bonus|Empowered Bonus; Grants +10 Mending while in Dungeons.|Empowered Bonus; Grants +10 Mending while in Dungeons.|Empowered Bonus; Grants +10 Mending while in Dungeons.|Empowered Bonus; Grants +10 Mending while in Dungeons.|Empowered Bonus; Grants +10 Mending while in Dungeons.|Empowered Bonus; Grants +10 Mending while in Dungeons.|Empowered Bonus; Grants +10 Mending while in Dungeons.|
|Cost to apply|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|
## Tips
- The [[Mending]] granted by [[Reforging|Empowered]] increases outgoing healing from abilities such as the [[Healer|Healing Circle]].
## Trivia
- Before 0.27.2, Sadan's Brooch used a recolored version of the [[Necromancer's Brooch]] texture.
  - The flavor text "Very original..." may refer to Sadan's Brooch using a recolored [[Necromancer's Brooch]] texture before 0.27.2, as well as the base stats of the [[Reforging|Empowered]] reforge being identical to those of the [[Reforging|Titanic]] reforge.
- The [[Reforging|Empowered]] reforge grants the same base [[Health]] and [[Defense]] as the [[Reforging|Titanic]] reforge, with an additional [[Mending]] bonus in [[Dungeons]].

Item ID: SALTY_SPRAYONATOR
Tooltip: Ability: Configure LEFT CLICK Select a nozzle and material for spraying. Certain materials attract certain types of  Pests.; Selected Nozzle: Salty Selected Material: Compost Attracts: Earthworm, Mosquito; Ability: Spray RIGHT CLICK Sprays your selected material onto the Garden plot you are on. Lasts for 60m.; Salty Nozzle Bonus:; ➜ +75 Bonus Pest Chance while in a sprayed plot.; ➜  Pests are 3x more likely to spawn in sprayed plots.; Cooldown: 3s; ❣ Requires Garden Level 5.; EPIC
A Salty Sprayonator is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] item.
## Obtaining
A Salty Sprayonator can be purchased from [[The Garden|SkyMart]] for 1 [[Juicy Sprayonator]], 1 [[Salty Nozzle]], and 10,000 [[Copper]].
## Usage
Sprayonator:
Salty Sprayonator
[Effects]
[Ability: Configure LEFT CLICK; Select a nozzle and material for spraying. Certain materials attract certain types of Pest (Pest (Mob Type)). Ability: Spray RIGHT CLICK; Sprays your selected material onto the Garden plot you are on. Lasts for 60m.; ➜ +75 Bonus Pest Chance while in a sprayed plot.; ➜ Pest (Pest (Mob Type)) are 3x more likely to spawn in sprayed plots.; Cooldown: 3s]
The Salty Sprayonator can be used to spray a [[The Garden|Garden]] plot for 60 minutes using [[Compost]], [[Plant Matter]], [[Dung]], [[Honey Jar]], [[Tasty Cheese]], or [[Jelly]].
### Attracting Pests
The Salty Sprayonator can be used with the appropriate materials to attract certain [[Pests]] to plots. When a spray is active, [[Pests]] are guaranteed to spawn in the plot the player is farming in, or one of the four directly adjacent plots.
The materials below can be used to attract specific pests:
|Material|Attracted Pests|
|-|-|
|[[Compost]]|[[Earthworm]], [[Mosquito]]|
|[[Plant Matter]]|[[Locust]], [[Slug]]|
|[[Dung]]|[[Beetle]], [[Fly]]|
|[[Honey Jar]]|[[Moth]], [[Cricket]]|
|[[Tasty Cheese]]|[[Rat]], [[Mite]]|
|[[Jelly]]|[[Praying Mantis]], [[Dragonfly]], [[Firefly]]|
The weights of the targeted [[Pests]] are increased by +1,000, on top of the base 100 weight.
### Nozzles
Additional effects are applied in sprayed plots depending on the selected nozzle:
- Basic Nozzle: Consumes 1 of the material. Grants +25 [[Bonus Pest Chance]] while in a sprayed plot. Pests are 1.5x more likely to spawn in sprayed plots.
- [[Juicy Nozzle]]: Consumes 3 of the material. Grants +50 [[Bonus Pest Chance]] while in a sprayed plot. Pests are 2x more likely to spawn in sprayed plots.
- [[Salty Nozzle]]: Consumes 5 of the material. Grants +75 [[Bonus Pest Chance]] while in a sprayed plot. Pests are 3x more likely to spawn in sprayed plots.
### Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Normal|SPRAYONATOR! You sprayed Plot - 1 with 5 Honey Jar! SPRAYONATOR! This will expire in 60m!|
|Used too recently|This plot was sprayed with that item recently! Try again soon!|
|Used in the Barn|You cannot spray a Barn plot!|
|Used outside of a plot|You can only spray within a plot!|

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: APEX_PRAEDATOR
Tooltip: Hunting Tool; Damage: +20; Ability: Absorptio RIGHT CLICK This weapon lets you absorb 20% of the basic combat stats of any weapon.; Ability: Vis Temperata Each strike of this weapon has its damage capped at 33% of the enemy's max Health. This weapon cannot cause a fatal blow.; Supra omnes, Apex Praedator solus regnat.; This item can be reforged!; EPIC AXE
A Savage Huntaxe - Praedator is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Huntaxes|Huntaxe]] crafted at [[Vinesap|Vinesap VII (7)]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Vinesap VII (7) ([[Vinesap]])|1x [[Reinforced Huntaxe - Cursus]] 3x [[Enchanted Vinesap]] 32x [[Stretching Sticks]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Vinesap]] ×3 + [[Reinforced Huntaxe - Cursus]] + [[Stretching Sticks]] x32 → Savage Huntaxe - Praedator|
## Usage
The Savage Huntaxe - Praedator has two abilities:
- Absorptio allows the Savage Huntaxe - Praedator to absorb 20% of the basic [[Stats|Combat Stats]] ([[Damage]], [[Strength]], [[Crit Damage]], etc) of any weapon.
- Vis Temperata caps the [[Damage]] of the Savage Huntaxe - Praedator at 33% of the enemy's max health and prevents the user from killing the enemy. This allows one to easily syphon their [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]] with a [[Small Pocket Black Hole|Small]] or [[Medium Pocket Black Hole]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Prime Huntaxe - Nex Titanum]]|Vinesap IX (9) ([[Vinesap]])|[[Savage Huntaxe - Praedator]] 1x Savage Huntaxe - Praedator 6x [[Enchanted Vinesap]] 64x [[Stretching Sticks]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Vinesap]] x2 ×3 + Savage Huntaxe - Praedator + [[Stretching Sticks]] x64 → [[Prime Huntaxe - Nex Titanum]]|
## Trivia
- The text in the Huntaxe's description, "Supra omnes, Apex Praedator solus regnat." is Latin, and means "Above all, the apex predator rules alone."
  - The item's name, "Praedator", means either "Raider", "Plunderer", "Pillager", "Hunter" or in biological contexts, "Predator". Both of those likely refer to the Starborns.
  - The ability name "Vis Temperata" means "Tempered force".

As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

A research on how to preserve and enhance class abilities in death.
Scarf's Thesis is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Accessories|Accessory]]. It is the upgraded form of [[Scarf's Studies]] and is unlocked after completion of [[The Catacombs - Floor II|Floor II]]. Additionally, one is rewarded at [[Scarf|Scarf II (2)]].
## Obtaining
### Boss Collection
Scarf's Thesis can be obtained as a reward from [[Scarf|Scarf II (2)]] after defeating [[Scarf]] 50 times.
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|4x [[Scarf's Studies]]|Recipe: [[Scarf's Studies]] ×4 → Scarf's Thesis|
## Usage
Accessory:
Scarf's Thesis
[Effects]
[Grants +4% Dungeons Class (Classes) experience.]
Accessory:
Scarf's Thesis (Rift) ([[Scarf's Thesis]]) [[Rift Time]]: +11
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Scarf's Grimoire]]|None|[[Scarf's Thesis]] 4x Scarf's Thesis|Recipe: Scarf's Thesis ×4 → [[Scarf's Grimoire]]|
### Salvaging
Scarf's Thesis can be salvaged for 60 [[Wither Essence]].
### Rift Transfer
Being [[Rift Dimension|# Rift-Transferable #]], Scarf's Thesis can be transferred into the [[Rift Dimension]]. There, it grants +11s [[Rift Time]].

# Scorched Topaz
Aliases: Heated, Hot Stuff
Categories: Epic items, Reforge stones, Hard Stone Collection, Forged items
[Scorched Topaz]
Type: Reforge Stone ([[Reforge Stones]])
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
[Reforge/Power Stone]
Reforge: Heated
Applied To: Mining Tools ([[Tools]])
Apply Cost: 250,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 73,728x (1,152 stacks (73,728 items total)) [[Hard Stone]] 512,000x (8,000 stacks (512,000 items total)) Rough Topaz Gemstone ([[Gemstone]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: HOT_STUFF
Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Heated reforge when combined with a Mining Tool.; Oooh, das hot!; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Mining Speed  Mining Fortune; Heated Bonus Grants +1 Mining Fortune and +4 Mining Speed per 1♨ Heat you have.; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; EPIC REFORGE STONE
Scorched Topaz is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] that applies the [[Reforging|Heated]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to [[Pickaxes]], [[Drills]] and the [[Gemstone Gauntlet]]. It grants [[Mining Fortune]] and [[Mining Speed]].
## Obtaining
### Forging
Scorched Topaz can be forged once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain IV (4)]] and [[Hard Stone|Hard Stone IV (4)]]. It requires 128 (2 stacks) [[Enchanted Hard Stone]], 1 [[Gemstone|Flawless Topaz Gemstone]], and 6 hours to forge.
#### Forging Tree
- [[Scorched Topaz]] 1 Scorched Topaz
  - 128 [[Enchanted Hard Stone]] 73,728 (1,152 stacks) [[Hard Stone]]
  - 1 [[Gemstone|Flawless Topaz Gemstone]] 80 (1 stack plus 16) [[Gemstone|Fine Topaz Gemstone]] 6,400 (100 stacks) [[Gemstone|Flawed Topaz Gemstone]] 512,000 (8,000 stacks) [[Gemstone|Rough Topaz Gemstone]]
## Usage
### Reforging
Scorched Topaz can be applied to [[Pickaxes]], [[Drills]] and the [[Gemstone Gauntlet]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Heated]] reforge, to grant [[Mining Fortune]] and [[Mining Speed]], which scale with the Mining Tool's rarity. This has no [[Mining]] requirement.
Additionally, it grants the Heated Bonus, which grants an additional 1 [[Mining Fortune]] and 4 [[Mining Speed]] per player's [[Heat]].
|Stat Bonus|Rarity COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity RARE ([[Rarity]])|Rarity EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Rarity MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity DIVINE ([[Rarity]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Stat Bonus|+3 Mn.Frt ([[Mining Fortune]]) +5 M.Spd ([[Mining Speed]])|+6 Mn.Frt ([[Mining Fortune]]) +10 M.Spd ([[Mining Speed]])|+9 Mn.Frt ([[Mining Fortune]]) +15 M.Spd ([[Mining Speed]])|+13 Mn.Frt ([[Mining Fortune]]) +25 M.Spd ([[Mining Speed]])|+16 Mn.Frt ([[Mining Fortune]]) +40 M.Spd ([[Mining Speed]])|+20 Mn.Frt ([[Mining Fortune]]) +55 M.Spd ([[Mining Speed]])|+25 Mn.Frt ([[Mining Fortune]]) +70 M.Spd ([[Mining Speed]])|
|Reforge Bonus|Heated Bonus; Grants +1 Mining Fortune and +4 Mining Speed per 1 Heat you have.|Heated Bonus; Grants +1 Mining Fortune and +4 Mining Speed per 1 Heat you have.|Heated Bonus; Grants +1 Mining Fortune and +4 Mining Speed per 1 Heat you have.|Heated Bonus; Grants +1 Mining Fortune and +4 Mining Speed per 1 Heat you have.|Heated Bonus; Grants +1 Mining Fortune and +4 Mining Speed per 1 Heat you have.|Heated Bonus; Grants +1 Mining Fortune and +4 Mining Speed per 1 Heat you have.|Heated Bonus; Grants +1 Mining Fortune and +4 Mining Speed per 1 Heat you have.|
|Cost to apply|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|
## Tips
- This reforge is only recommended, when the user prioritizes mining in the [[Magma Fields]], as [[Reforging|Auspicious]] provides better stats when the bonus doesn't apply.
## Trivia
- Scorched Topaz was formerly called Hot Stuff.
- The tooltip "Oooh, das hot!" may be a reference to YouTube Rewind 2018, where American actor Will Smith says the quote upon spotting a flying bus.
- Scorched Topaz's texture used to be similar to Twisted Hypno Steve.

## Obtaining
### Fusion
A Sea Serpent Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]]. For [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]], the following input conditions must be met:
- First: [[Moray Eel Shard]]
- Second: [[Rarity|RARE]] or higher and Water Category
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Sea Serpent Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Echo of Hunter]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Echo of Hunter ([[Attributes]]); Your Hunter Attributes are +2%–20% stronger.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|

|Ⅲ (3)|2|4|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|2|6|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|3|9|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|3|12|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|4|16|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|4|20|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|5|25|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|7|32|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Sea Serpent Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

# Seal of the Family
Aliases: Seal Of The Family
Categories: Epic items, Accessories, Dark Auction
[Seal of the Family]
Type: Accessory ([[Accessories]])
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
[Upgrades]
← Previous: [[Crooked Artifact]]
Next →: None
[Item Properties]
Upgradeable: ✕ No
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Soulbound: ✔ Yes (Solo)
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SEAL_OF_THE_FAMILY
Tooltip: Accessory Power: +12; Get 3% off on most shops.; * Soulbound * Works while in Accessory Bag!; EPIC ACCESSORY
The Seal of the Family is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] that gives its user 3% off on most shops.
## Obtaining
### Shop Purchase
The Seal of the Family can be purchased from [[Lucius]] for 10,000,000 [[Coins]] and a [[Crooked Artifact]] after the [[Player]] had bought at least 7 items on the [[Dark Auction]].
The cumulative cost of purchasing a Seal of the Family, factoring out coins spent on unique Dark Auction purchases, is 12,500,000 [[Coins]] (10,000,000 + 2,000,000 + 500,000): this includes the cost of the [[Crooked Artifact]] and [[Shady Ring]].
If the player loses the item, it can be repurchased from [[Rusty]] in the [[Gold Mine]] for 10,000,000 [[Coins]].
## Usage
Accessory:
Seal of the Family
[Effects]
[Get 3% off on most shops.]
## Trivia
- The price-reducing effect of the Seal of the Family works with most NPC shops in the game.
  - Notably it doesn't work for NPCs that provide a service, rather than selling items such as: [[Vanessa]], [[Maddox the Slayer]], [[Kat]], [[Fann]], [[Blacksmith]], [[Geo]], [[Jotraeline Greatforge]], [[Treasure Hunter]], analyzing mutations at [[Jake]], [[Teddy]] and [[Freddy]], [[Jacobus]], [[Zarina]] and [[Roddy]].
  - Despite being accessible by, owned by or related to NPCs, it doesn't affect [[Auction House]], [[Bazaar]], [[Dark Auction]], [[Shen's Auction]], the [[Regalia Room]], recipes that cost [[Coins]] at [[The Forge|Forge]], opening [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Dungeon Reward Chests]] or [[Richard|Stonks Auction]].
    - On the other hand, it does affect [[The Garden|Skymart]], which isn't accessed via any NPCs.
  - Still, there are certain shops that aren't affected; known exceptions include: [[Bea]], [[Fann]], the [[Golden Dragon]], [[Grumblefoot]], the [[Jade Dragon]], [[Ludleth]], [[Oringo]], [[Rosetta]], [[Seymour]], [[Jax]], [[Odger]] and [[Researcher Ribery]]
    - Oddly, Certain shops are affected only partially. It affects all items in [[Brynmor]]'s shop except [[Fiesta Flask]] and [[Packrat Shard]]. Several items from [[Rusty]]'s shop aren't affected either.
  - It even works with [[Centauri]] on the [[Alpha Hypixel Network]], but only for costs above 1 [[Coins|Coin]], such as when trading a stack of items.
- Despite not being part of the item's description, it only affect [[Coins]] prices, and won't affect other currencies or the amounts of items needed to purchase something.
- The item model for the Seal of the Family is [[Sirius]]'s head.
- Obtaining this item from Lucius will unlock the [[Achievements|"Lifelong Contract" Achievement]].
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Dec 3, 2019|Added Seal of the Family.|
|Mar 28, 2023|0.18.2|Seal of the Family was added to [[Rusty]]'s shop and is now Co-op Soulbound ([[Soulbound]]).|
|Nov 28, 2023|0.19.8|Changed from Co-op Soulbound ([[Soulbound]]) to [[Soulbound]].|
|Oct 6, 2026|0.27.2|Changed purchase requirement from 15 [[Dark Auction]] purchases to 7.|

The Shadow Assassin Cloak is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Dungeons|Dungeon]] [[Cloaks|Cloak]].
## Obtaining
### Dungeon Reward Chests
The Shadow Assassin Cloak can be obtained from [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Dungeon Reward Chests]] in [[The Catacombs - Floor V]].
|Catacombs Floor ([[Catacombs]])|Chest ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|Cost; (Chest + Added)|Average Chance; (No Bonuses)|Average Chance; (S+ (Score above 300 points.) ([[Dungeon Score]]) Max Bonuses ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]]))|Quality ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|Weight ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Floor V ([[The Catacombs - Floor V]])|Obsidian|2,500,000 [[Coins]]|1.1834% (1 in 84.5 runs)|2.0091% (1 in 49.8 runs)|275|4|
|Floor V ([[The Catacombs - Floor V]])|Bedrock|2,500,000 [[Coins]]|2.0203% (1 in 49.5 runs)|2.5669% (1 in 39 runs)|275|5|
|Floor V ([[The Catacombs - Floor V]]) (Master Mode)|Obsidian|2,500,000 [[Coins]]|1.173% (1 in 85.3 runs)|2.1471% (1 in 46.6 runs)|275|4|
|Floor V ([[The Catacombs - Floor V]]) (Master Mode)|Bedrock|2,500,000 [[Coins]]|2.3663% (1 in 42.3 runs)|3.3889% (1 in 29.5 runs)|275|5|
## Usage
Cloak:
Shadow Assassin Cloak [[Strength]]: +20 [[Speed]]: +3
[Effects]
[On teleport: Your next melee hit within 5s deals 10% more damage.]
Cloak:
[[Shadow Assassin Cloak]] [[Strength]]: +25 [[Speed]]: +5
[Effects]
[On teleport: Your next melee hit within 5s deals 10% more damage.]
The Shadow Assassin Cloak, similar to [[Shadow Assassin Armor]] but separate from its full set, grants the user a bonus upon teleporting: upon teleporting, the user's next hit deals 10% more damage.
## Upgrading
A Shadow Assassin Cloak can be upgraded into Shadow Assassin Cloak using 8 [[Livid Fragment|Livid Fragments]] to buff its stats, granting:
- +5 [[Strength]]
- +2 [[Speed]]
- Increases rarity from [[Rarity|EPIC]] to [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]]
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|[[Shadow Assassin Cloak]] 1x Shadow Assassin Cloak 8x [[Livid Fragment]]|Recipe: [[Livid Fragment]] ×8 + Shadow Assassin Cloak → [[Shadow Assassin Cloak]]|

## Obtaining
### Shop Purchase
Shadow Goggles can be bought from [[Ophelia]] for 800,000 [[Coins]] and 1 [[Dark Goggles]] upon completion of [[The Catacombs - Floor III]].
## Usage
Helmet:
Shadow Goggles [[Intelligence]]: +200 [[Ability Damage]]: +35
### Shop Purchase
Shadow Goggles can be upgraded into [[Wither Goggles]] at [[Ophelia]].

This item is commonly used for the [[Mage]] Class as it grants +200 [[Intelligence]] and +35 [[Ability Damage]]. It is a staple for mage sets and is recommended to use it when requirements are met.

The Shaman Sword is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Swords|Sword]]. It is unlocked at [[Wolf Slayer|Wolf Slayer III (3)]] and is the predecessor to the [[Pooch Sword]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Wolf Slayer III (3) ([[Wolf Slayer]])|2x [[Golden Tooth]] 4x [[Enchanted Bone]] 8x [[Enchanted Iron Ingot]]|Recipe: [[Golden Tooth]] x2 + [[Enchanted Iron Ingot]] x8 + [[Enchanted Bone]] x4 → Shaman Sword|
## Usage
Sword:
Shaman Sword [[Damage]]: +100 [[Strength]]: +20 [[Speed]]: +5
[Effects]
[Deal +10 Damage per Wolf Slayer level.; Deal +100% Damage against Wolves; Receive -20% Damage from Animal mobs.; Gain +30 Combat Wisdom against Animal mobs.]
The Shaman Sword deals +10 [[Damage]] per [[Wolf Slayer]] level, alongside granting +100% [[Damage]] against wolves and +30 [[Combat Wisdom]] against [[Animal]] mobs. Additionally, while holding the Shaman Sword, the wielder receives -20% damage from [[Animal]] mobs.
### Crafting

## Trivia
- The Shaman Sword used to pair well with [[Mastiff Armor]], due to the old ability scaling damage with [[Health]].

# Shellfruit
Categories: Epic items, Items in Sacks, Items, Mutations
[Shellfruit]
Type: Mutation
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Small Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Medium Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Large Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SHELLFRUIT
Tooltip: When the shell cracks open, sweet, refreshing juice spills out, revealing the fruit inside.; Analyze this Mutation at the Crop Analyzer in Jake's Underground Lab.; EPIC MUTATION
A Shellfruit is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Mutations|Mutation]].
## Obtaining
A Shellfruit can be obtained by placing [[Turtlellini]] in the [[Greenhouse]] and exploding it with 2 [[Blastberry|Blastberries]]. Each [[Blastberry]] requires 1 Growth Stage to be primed. [[Blastberry|Blastberries]] blow up all 8 adjacent crops, meaning up to 8 Shellfruits can be harvested.
When placed near another crop, Shellfruit retains water by 50% and grants immunity to negative effects.
## Crop Drops
When harvested, Shellfruits drop 400 (6 stacks plus 16) [[Cocoa Beans]] and +800 [[Farming|Farming XP]].
## Usage
### Crop Analyzer
A Shellfruit can be analyzed in the [[Crop Analyzer]] for 500,000 [[Coins]]. When analyzed, it grants 250 [[Copper]]. The first time analyzing a Shellfruit grants an additional 1,000 [[Copper]].
### Mutations
Shellfruit are used to create a [[Phantomleaf]] and [[Timestalk]].
## Gallery
- Shellfruit in the Greenhouse Shellfruit in the [[Greenhouse]]
## History

|Dec 5, 2025|Added Shellfruit.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Dec 15, 2025|0.24|Added Shellfruit.|
|Feb 2, 2026|Reduced the drops from Shellfruit by 5 times.|
|Aug 24, 2026|Removed the 800 (12 stacks plus 32) [[Melon Slice]] drops from Shellfruit.|

A Shellwise Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Shellwise]] with a [[Fishing Nets|Fishing Net]].

### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Shellwise Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Shell]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:

As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Silkbreeze Shard can be obtained from killing a [[Silkbreeze]]. Since it is an [[Elusive]] mob, it is guaranteed to be [[Charm Chance|Charmed]] and drop one.
### Fusion
A Silkbreeze Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Silkbreeze Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Forager's Fortune]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Forager's Fortune ([[Attributes]]); Grants +0.5–5 [[Magic Find]] while on Foraging Islands.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|1|2|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|2|4|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|2|6|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|3|9|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|3|12|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|4|16|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|4|20|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|5|25|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|7|32|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Silkbreeze Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
[Data from SkyShards.]
## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Silkbreeze Shard has the following description:
- It serves as a lookout of sorts for the forest in which it resides, moving so fast that it can detect and warn of imminent threats before it's even spotted.

Silver Magmafish is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] item unlocked at [[Magmafish|Magmafish III (3)]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting

|-|-|-|
|Magmafish III (3) ([[Magmafish]])|80x [[Magmafish]]|Recipe: [[Magmafish]] x16 ×7 → Silver Magmafish|

|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|

|[Lv597]|[[Lord Jawbus]]|16-32|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|

### Fishing
Silver Magmafish can rarely be fished as a [[Treasure|GOOD CATCH]] in [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Lava]] on the [[Crimson Isle]].
### Seal's Treat Bag
4x Silver Magmafish have a 5.5% (1 in 18) chance to be obtained from a [[Seal's Treat Bag]].
### Odger
[[Odger]] rewards 20x Silver Magmafish for their Adept [[Trophy Fish|Trophy Fishing]] reward. Silver Magmafish is also given when filleting [[Odger|Trophy Fish]].

# Sinful Dice
Categories: Epic items, Items, Crafting ingredients, Slayer, Enderman Slayer, Mob Drops
[Sinful Dice]
Type: Crafting Item
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
Tooltip Text: Snake eyes!
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SINFUL_DICE
Tooltip: Snake eyes!; Right-click to view recipes!; EPIC
The Sinful Dice is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] crafting item. It is an [[Drop Chance|7Extraordinary]] drop obtained from the [[Voidgloom Seraph]] Tier IV. It is used to craft the [[Sinseeker Scythe]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
Sinful Dice has a 0.47% (1/217.98) to drop from Tier IV [[Voidgloom Seraph]] if the player has [[Enderman Slayer|Enderman Slayer LVL VI (6)]].
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Sinseeker Scythe]]|Enderman Slayer VI (6) ([[Enderman Slayer]])|[[Sinful Dice]] 1x Sinful Dice 16x [[Null Atom]] 32x [[Null Ovoid]]|Recipe: [[Null Atom]] x4 ×4 + [[Null Ovoid]] x8 ×4 + Sinful Dice → [[Sinseeker Scythe]]|
## Trivia
- The Sinful Dice head texture is a re-skin of the [[Lucky Dice]] texture, and is likely meant to be the opposite of the Lucky Dice.
- [[Sinful Dice]] Sinful Dice, [[Archfiend Dice]] and [[High Class Archfiend Dice]] are one of few items in the game whose texture varies between items. Each has 6 texture variants, one for each side of the die.
  - Unlike all other items, [[Lucky Dice]] only has one. It is also the only Dice that got a new texture in the SkyBlock Resource Pack.
    - Oddly enough, the old sprite of [[Lucky Dice]], which had 5 pips on top is different than the sprite of 5 pips on top of any other Dice item.
    - Despite there being only 6 different sprites of Dice items, there are actually 48 possible variants of shown sides of a standard dice from an angle where 3 sides are shown.
  - When this item has been dropped on the ground and picked back up by a player, or when switched away from and back to in the hotbar, it will change its texture randomly to one of its six texture variants.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|May 31, 2021|0.11.5|Added Sinful Dice.|

Tooltip: Damage: +100; Strength: +100; Ability: Sinrecall Transmission RIGHT CLICK Zap a line 4 blocks forward.; Recast within 1s (1.5x mana) or warp back to starting point.; Mobs crossing the line(s) receive a melee hit from what you're holding.; Mana Cost: 260; This item can be reforged!; ☠ Requires Enderman Slayer 6.; EPIC SWORD
The Sinseeker Scythe is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Swords|Sword]] unlocked at [[Enderman Slayer|Enderman Slayer VI (6)]]. Its ability teleports the player, then recalls them and damages all enemies along the path.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Enderman Slayer VI (6) ([[Enderman Slayer]])|1x [[Sinful Dice]] 16x [[Null Atom]] 32x [[Null Ovoid]]|Recipe: [[Null Atom]] x4 ×4 + [[Null Ovoid]] x8 ×4 + [[Sinful Dice]] → Sinseeker Scythe|
## Usage
Sword:
Sinseeker Scythe [[Damage]]: +100 [[Strength]]: +100
[Effects]
[Ability: Sinrecall Transmission RIGHT CLICK; Zap a line 4 blocks forward.; Recast within 1s (1.5x Mana) or warp back to starting point.; Mobs crossing the line(s) receive a melee hit from what you're holding.; Mana Cost (Intelligence): 260]
When the ability is used, it teleports the player 4 blocks forward, leaving behind a red line. For every time the ability is recast within 1 second, its [[Intelligence|Mana Cost]] increases by 1.5x.
If the ability is not recast within 1 second, the user will be teleported back to where they started. All enemies in contact with the red line(s) receive a melee hit from the weapon the user holds.
Its ability can be upgraded with [[Transmission Tuner|Transmission Tuners]].
## Essence Crafting
This weapon can be upgraded with Dragon Essence. Once upgraded, it requires [[Catacombs|Catacombs XXIII (23)]] to use.
Essence Required; [[Dragon Essence]]:
|Action|Cost|Cumul. (Cumulative)|
|-|-|-|
|Conversion (The cost of the conversion to a dungeon item.)|250|250|
|✪|70|320|
|✪✪|140|460|
|✪✪✪|210|670|
|✪✪✪✪|350; 10,000 [[Coins]]|1,020; 10,000 [[Coins]]|
|✪✪✪✪✪|600; 25,000 [[Coins]]|1,620; 35,000 [[Coins]]|
## Trivia
- This item can be used to achieve very fast completions for [[Island Racing|Races]] if the user has enough [[Mana]] to be able to continuously use its Sinrecall Transmission ability.
- The Sinseeker Scythe is often used for obtaining [[Dungeon Secrets]] faster, because it can be used to quickly teleport forward and backward, saving time.
- If one gets low on [[Health]] in combat, the Sinseeker Scythe can be useful as it can mark the current location before dying and teleport to the location after dying.
## Gallery
- In-hand model.

# Sleeping Eye
Categories: Epic items, Items, Ender
[Sleeping Eye]
Type: Item
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SLEEPING_EYE
Tooltip: Keep this item in your inventory to recover your placed Summoning Eye when you leave or when you click the Ender Altar.; This item becomes imbued with the magic of the Dragon when it spawns, turning it into a Remnant of the Eye.; EPIC
A Sleeping Eye is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] item.
## Usage
A Sleeping Eye replaces a [[Summoning Eye]] in the player's inventory when it is placed. It serves as a placeholder and reverts back to a [[Summoning Eye]] when it is withdrawn, reducing the amount of [[Summoning Eye|Summoning Eyes]] placed by 1: other than that, it is purely a placeholder item. [[Summoning Eye|Summoning Eyes]] can only be withdrawn every 3 seconds.
After placing all 8 [[Summoning Eye|Summoning Eyes]], all Sleeping Eyes in the player's inventory convert to [[Remnant of the Eye|Remnants of the Eye]].
## Bugs
- On unique and rare occasions, a Sleeping Eye in the [[Player|inventory]] without returning the [[Summoning Eye]]. The item can then be [[Auction House|auctioned]]. This behavior can be replicated by putting a sleeping eye in the [[Storage]]. When leaving [[The End]], if a Sleeping Eye is in the [[Storage]], it will not be replaced and can be removed. Note that the [[Summoning Eye]] will be lost.

The Slime Bow is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Bows|Bow]] that is unlocked at [[Slimeball|Slimeball IX (9)]]. It consumes [[Slimeball|Slimeballs]] to deal bonus damage to [[Magma Cube|Magma Cubes]] and all [[Slime (disambiguation)|Slimes]].

### Crafting

Bow:

When Slimeballs are in the inventory or Quiver, the Slime Bow consumes 1 Slimeball alongside an [[Arrows|Arrow]] per shot. Slimes and [[Magma Cube|Magma Cubes]] take 5x damage from the arrow.

## Obtaining
The Snow Owl Skin could be purchased from the Winter 2023 [[Taylor|Seasonal Bundle]] from [[Taylor]]. Being [[Rarity|EPIC]], it cost 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]]. However, due to being before the Winter 2024 Bundle, it cannot appear in future bundles, making it now unobtainable.

Snow Suit is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Armor]] set obtainable through opening [[Gifts]]. Each piece grants a 5% bonus gift chance on every present earned from the [[Defend Jerry's Workshop]] event during the [[Season of Jerry]], adding up to a 20% bonus when wearing the full set (including the equipment pieces).
## Obtaining
### [[Gifts]]
|Armor Piece|Gifts White|Gifts Green|Gifts Red|
|-|-|-|-|
|Helmet|0.03% (1 in 3324.5 gifts)|0.06% (1 in 1625.75 gifts)|0.64% (1 in 155.06 gifts)|
|Chestplate|0.05% (1 in 2216.33 gifts)|0.09% (1 in 1083.83 gifts)|0.64% (1 in 155.06 gifts)|
|Leggings|0.05% (1 in 2216.33 gifts)|0.09% (1 in 1083.83 gifts)|0.64% (1 in 155.06 gifts)|
|Boots|0.03% (1 in 3324.5 gifts)|0.06% (1 in 1625.75 gifts)|0.64% (1 in 155.06 gifts)|
### Equipment
The [[Snow Necklace]], [[Snow Cloak|Cloak]], [[Snow Belt|Belt]] and [[Snow Gloves|Gloves]] can be purchased from [[Einary]], for 10 [[North Stars]] each.
## Usage
Armor Set:
|Helmet|Chestplate|
|-|-|
|Snow Suit Helmet ([[Snow Suit Armor]]); [[Health]]: +70 [[Defense]]: +30 Each piece grants +5% bonus gift chance for every present you earn from the Gift Attack ([[Defend Jerry's Workshop]]) minigame!; All stats of this armor piece are doubled on [[Jerry's Workshop]]!|Snow Suit Chestplate ([[Snow Suit Armor]]); [[Health]]: +100 [[Defense]]: +40 Each piece grants +5% bonus gift chance for every present you earn from the Gift Attack ([[Defend Jerry's Workshop]]) minigame!; All stats of this armor piece are doubled on [[Jerry's Workshop]]!|
|Leggings|Boots|
|Snow Suit Leggings ([[Snow Suit Armor]]); [[Health]]: +75 [[Defense]]: +30 Each piece grants +5% bonus gift chance for every present you earn from the Gift Attack ([[Defend Jerry's Workshop]]) minigame!; All stats of this armor piece are doubled on [[Jerry's Workshop]]!|Snow Suit Boots ([[Snow Suit Armor]]); [[Health]]: +65 [[Defense]]: +25 Each piece grants +5% bonus gift chance for every present you earn from the Gift Attack ([[Defend Jerry's Workshop]]) minigame!; All stats of this armor piece are doubled on [[Jerry's Workshop]]!|
[Total]
[Snow Suit Armor Health: +310 Defense: +125]
[Effects]
[Full Set Bonus: Cold Thumb; Allows the wearer to shoot unlimited snowballs when using Frosty the Snow Cannon/Frosty the Snow Blaster.]
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Nutcracker Helmet ([[Nutcracker Armor]])|None|1x Snow Suit Helmet ([[Snow Suit Armor]]) 15x [[Walnut]]|Recipe: [[Walnut]] x3 ×5 + [[Snow Suit Armor]] → [[Nutcracker Armor]]|
|Nutcracker Chestplate ([[Nutcracker Armor]])|None|1x Snow Suit Chestplate ([[Snow Suit Armor]]) 24x [[Walnut]]|Recipe: [[Walnut]] x3 ×8 + [[Snow Suit Armor]] → [[Nutcracker Armor]]|
|Nutcracker Leggings ([[Nutcracker Armor]])|None|1x Snow Suit Leggings ([[Snow Suit Armor]]) 21x [[Walnut]]|Recipe: [[Walnut]] x3 ×7 + [[Snow Suit Armor]] → [[Nutcracker Armor]]|
|Nutcracker Boots ([[Nutcracker Armor]])|None|1x Snow Suit Boots ([[Snow Suit Armor]]) 12x [[Walnut]]|Recipe: [[Walnut]] x3 ×4 + [[Snow Suit Armor]] → [[Nutcracker Armor]]|
### Salvaging
Snow Suit Armor can be salvaged for 10 [[Ice Essence]] per piece. Despite technically being part of the Snow Suit Armor tiered bonus, Snow Suit equipment cannot be salvaged.

|[[Snowglobe Skin]]|[[Snowglobe Skin]]|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|[[Fire Sale]] - December 25, 2020 - 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]]||
## Tips
- This armor setup is very useful during the [[Defend Jerry's Workshop]] event due to the [[Snowball]] bonus, allowing you to use abilities that aid you in the fight like the [[Aspect of the End]]'s ability, [[Power Orbs]], and [[Wand of Healing]].
## Trivia
- The armor set's Cold Thumb ability also works with [[Frosty the Snow Blaster]].
- There was an original texture for the helmet that looks like the helmet for [[Speedster Armor]].
- The Snow Suit Armor is one of 6 armor pieces which have the hex color code of ⁠ #FFFFFF. The others are [[Heavy Armor|Heavy Helmet]], [[Heavy Armor|Heavy Boots]], [[Armor of the Pack|Helmet of the Pack]], [[Kelly's T-Shirt]] and [[Leggings of the Coven]] (white variant).

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SOUL_WHIP
Tooltip: Damage: +145; Strength: +175; Gemstones: [⚔]; Deals 2x damage to Sea Creatures.; Ability: Flay RIGHT CLICK Flay your whip in an arc, dealing your melee damage to all enemies in its path.; This item can be reforged!; ❣ Requires Fishing Skill 26.; EPIC SWORD
The Soul Whip is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Swords|Sword]]. It requires [[Fishing|Fishing XXVI (26)]] to use.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|8x [[Soul Fragment]] 12x [[Ectoplasm]]|Recipe: [[Ectoplasm]] x4 ×3 + [[Soul Fragment]] x4 ×2 → Soul Whip|
## Usage
Sword:
Soul Whip [[Damage]]: +145 [[Strength]]: +175
[Effects]
[Deals 2x damage to Sea Creatures. Ability: Flay RIGHT CLICK; Flay your whip in an arc, dealing your melee damage to all enemies in its path.]
The Soul Whip can be used as a melee weapon or a ranged weapon.
When the Flay ability is used, a brown and black beam is shot in the direction the player is aiming. This beam has a parabolic trajectory, traveling forward 13 blocks and upwards 2 blocks. The beam travels through enemies, damaging all in its path; for the first 3 enemies hit, it deals its melee damage, then it is halved for every subsequent enemy hit. The ability does not consume [[Mana]], but has a hidden cooldown of 0.5s. Holding right-click will continuously activate the ability.
The Soul Whip also deals 2x damage to [[Sea Creatures]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Flaming Flay]]|None|[[Soul Whip]] 1x Soul Whip 1x [[Hotspot Water Orb]] 1x [[Brimstone Handle]] 1x [[Braided Griffin Feather]] 1x [[Lava Water Orb]]|Recipe: [[Braided Griffin Feather]] + Soul Whip + [[Hotspot Water Orb]] + [[Brimstone Handle]] + [[Lava Water Orb]] → [[Flaming Flay]]|
## Essence Crafting
The Soul Whip can be upgraded with [[Wither Essence]]. Once dungeonized, it requires [[Catacombs|Catacombs XXIII (23)]] to use.
Essence Required; [[Wither Essence]]:
|Action|Cost|Cumul. (Cumulative)|
|-|-|-|
|Conversion (The cost of the conversion to a dungeon item.)|300|300|
|✪|200|500|
|✪✪|300|800|
|✪✪✪|400|1,200|
|✪✪✪✪|550; 10,000 [[Coins]]|1,750; 10,000 [[Coins]]|
|✪✪✪✪✪|750; 25,000 [[Coins]]|2,500; 35,000 [[Coins]]|
## Tips
- Due to the low base [[Damage]] of the weapon, it is recommended to use the [[Reforging|Suspicious]] reforge on it.
## Trivia
- Melee-only enchantments, such [[Life Steal]], [[Mana Steal]], [[Sharpness]], and [[Drain]], work on the beam.
- When right-clicking with this weapon, the [[Autopet]] rule for throwing a fishing hook does not trigger.
- Like the [[Halberd of the Shredded]]'s Throw, when the whip projectile hits a mob, it is actually considered as that a melee hit is dealt by the player before the damage is affected by the ability's damage multiplier. This means that sword swap works with this weapon’s ability.
- Despite the hidden attack cooldown of 0.5s, the Soul Whip can attack faster if the player spams the ability instead of holding right click.
## Bugs
- While [[Power Scrolls]] can be applied to a Soul Whip, its ability does not activate them.

# Soulflow Engine
Categories: Epic items, Minion upgrades, Craftable items, Minion Modifiers, Slayer, Enderman Slayer
[Soulflow Engine]
Type: Minion Upgrade ([[Minion Upgrades]])
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
Obtained: [[Crafting]]
Function: Reduces the minion's output by 50%, but it will start generating Soulflow items.; While equipped on a Voidling minion, output is increased by 3% per minion level.
Slayer Level ([[Slayer]]) Requirement: Enderman Slayer V (5) ([[Enderman Slayer]])
[Upgrades]
← Previous: [[Lesser Soulflow Engine]]
Next →: None
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 11,264x (176 stacks (11,264 items total)) [[Null Sphere]] 450,560x (7,040 stacks (450,560 items total)) [[Obsidian]] 61,440x (960 stacks (61,440 items total)) [[Ender Pearl]] 6,144x (96 stacks (6,144 items total)) [[Blaze Rod]] 819,200x (12,800 stacks (819,200 items total)) [[Iron Ingot]] 2x [[Null Atom]] 30,720x (480 stacks (30,720 items total)) [[Titanium]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SOULFLOW_ENGINE
Tooltip: Minion Upgrade; This item can be used as a minion upgrade.; Placing it in a minion will reduce the minion's output by 50%, but it will start generating Soulflow items.; While equipped on a Voidling minion, output is increased by 3% per minion level.; ☠ Requires Enderman Slayer 5.; EPIC
The Soulflow Engine is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Minion Upgrades|Minion Upgrade]] that is unlocked at [[Enderman Slayer|Enderman Slayer V (5)]]. It sacrifices [[Minions|minion]] output for [[Soulflow (Mechanic)|Soulflow]] item generation. If it is added to a [[Voidling Minion]], the [[Raw Soulflow]] output will increase by 3% per minion tier.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Enderman Slayer V (5) ([[Enderman Slayer]])|2x [[Null Atom]] 4x [[Lesser Soulflow Engine]] 12x [[Refined Titanium]] 64x [[Null Ovoid]]|Recipe: [[Null Atom]] ×2 + [[Lesser Soulflow Engine]] ×4 + [[Null Ovoid]] x64 + [[Refined Titanium]] x6 ×2 → Soulflow Engine|
## Usage
### Minion Upgrade
The Soulflow Engine requires [[Enderman Slayer|Enderman Slayer V (5)]] to use.
Soulflow Engine can be placed in all [[Minions]]. When it is added to a minion, the minion's output will be reduced by 50%, but the minion will produce 1 [[Raw Soulflow]] every 90 seconds when player is on the island and every 105 seconds when not. If it is added to a [[Voidling Minion]], the [[Raw Soulflow]] output will increase by 3% per minion tier.
The [[Raw Soulflow]] production cooldown isn't affected by minion speed modifiers. Drop multiplier [[Minion Fuel|Minion Fuels]] (like [[Hyper Catalyst]]) do increase the amount of [[Raw Soulflow]] made, including the extra [[Raw Soulflow]] from the boost of [[Voidling Minion]]. Purple particles will appear above minions with a Soulflow Engine.
## Expected Profits
The following is the expected revenue from 1 minion with a Soulflow Engine, based on the current [[Bazaar (NPC)|Bazaar]] prices for [[Soulflow (Item)|Soulflow]] and [[Raw Soulflow]].
| [[Super Compactor 3000]]|
|-|
|✕|
|✔|
## Trivia
- Minions will wait until the next production cycle to generate [[Raw Soulflow]] if the player is on the island.
- A single minion cannot use 2 Soulflow Engines at the same time.
- This item produces [[Raw Soulflow]] in about 2x speed of the [[Lesser Soulflow Engine|previous tier]].
- If a minion with a [[Budget Hopper]] or [[Enchanted Hopper]] is full while holding a Soulflow Engine, the produced [[Raw Soulflow]] will be deleted.
- If the Soulflow Engine is used for purely getting soulflow, a good choice for minion to use with the upgrade is [[Voidling Minion]] or a minion that does not fill their storage quickly with their own drops. This leaves more room for soulflow instead. For example:
  - [[Inferno Minion]], which is the slowest minion in the game if not given [[Inferno Minion Fuel]]
  - [[Vampire Minion]], which is the second slowest minion in the game
  - A [[Super Compactor 3000]] with any slow minion whose drops have a second compacted form, e.g. [[Emerald Minion]], [[Quartz Minion]], [[Blaze Minion]].
- If the produced soulflow is used for profit, it is better to use a minion that already makes good profit by itself.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|May 31, 2021|0.11.5|Added Soulflow Engine.|
|Apr 18, 2023|0.18.3|Added Soulflow Engine to the [[Bazaar]].|

Gloves:
Soulweaver Gloves [[Strength]]: +10 [[Crit Damage]]: +10
[Effects]
[While in The Catacombs (Catacombs), summon a Haunted Skull that slowly revolves around you every 15 seconds or when you kill a mob with melee damage.; When an enemy is hit by a Haunted Skull, they are stunned for 2 seconds and deal 5% less damage.]
The Soulweaver Gloves, while in the [[Catacombs]], summons a Haunted Skull that revolves around the player every 15s or when a mob is killed through melee damage. Mobs hit by a Haunted Skull are stunned for 2s and deal 5% less damage. A maximum of 3 Haunted Skulls can be summoned at a time[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)].

The Special Zealot Shard is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Special Upgrades]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Special Zealot Shard can be obtained from killing a [[Special Zealot]]. Since it is an [[Elusive]] mob, it is guaranteed to be [[Charm Chance|Charmed]] and drop one.
### Fusion
A Special Zealot Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].

### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Special Zealot Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Special Upgrades]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Special Upgrades ([[Attributes]]); Summoning Eyes ([[Summoning Eye]]) you drop have a +0.5%–5% chance to be recombobulated.; Placing recombobulated Summoning Eyes increases your chance of spawning a Superior Dragon
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

## Obtaining
The Spelling Bee Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on February 24, 2024. Being [[Rarity|EPIC]], it cost 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 1,500 purchases.

The Spelling Bee Skin can be applied to the player's summoned [[Bee Pet]] by right-clicking it to change its particle trail and appearance.

The Sphinx Shard is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Mythological Tracker]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Sphinx Shard can be obtained from killing a [[Sphinx]]. Since it is an [[Elusive]] mob, it is guaranteed to be [[Charm Chance|Charmed]] and drop one.
### Fusion
A Sphinx Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Sphinx Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Mythological Tracker]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Mythological Tracker ([[Attributes]]); Grants +0.5–5 [[Tracking]] on [[Griffin Burrows]].
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|1|2|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|2|4|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|2|6|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|3|9|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|3|12|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|4|16|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|4|20|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|5|25|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|7|32|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Sphinx Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

||

## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Sphinx Shard has the following description:
- Sometimes a simple question is all that lies between a long, fulfilling life and an imminent death.

The Spider Artifact is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] that reduces the damage taken from [[Arthropod]] mobs by 15%. It requires [[Spider Slayer|Spider Slayer VI (6)]]. It is the upgraded form of the [[Spider Ring]] and is the final form of the [[Spider Talisman]].

### Crafting

## Usage
Accessory:
Spider Artifact
[Effects]
[Reduces the damage taken from Arthropod mobs by 15%.]
## Trivia
- Prior to the October 5, 2021 Update, there was a bug that allowed the Spider Talisman, Spider Ring, and Spider Artifact to be stacked to get a total of 30% damage reduction.

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: STARRED_SPIDER_QUEENS_STINGER
Tooltip: Damage: +360; Arrows shot using this bow have an aura around them that deals 360 Damage to nearby enemies instead of dealing impact damage. Arrows travel through enemies.; This item is Arachnal !; This item can be reforged!; EPIC BOW
The Spider Queen's Stinger is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Bows|Bow]] crafted with [[Arachne]] resources. Its [[Arrows]] have an aura around them dealing damage to nearby [[Mob|Enemies]] based on [[Strength]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|6x [[Enchanted Spider Eye]] 12x [[Enchanted Dark Oak Log]] 48x [[Soul String]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Dark Oak Log]] x6 ×2 + [[Soul String]] x16 ×3 + [[Enchanted Spider Eye]] x6 → Spider Queen's Stinger|
## Usage
Bow:
Spider Queen's Stinger [[Damage]]: +360
[Effects]
[Arrows shot using this bow have an aura around them that deals 360 Damage to nearby enemies instead of dealing impact damage. Arrows travel through enemies.]
The Spider Queen's Stinger shoots special arrows with a damaging aura about them. These special arrows travel through enemies but not walls. Enemies within 2.5 blocks of the arrow take 360+(3.6×Strength) [[True Damage]]. The arrow is not affected by [[Hot Potato Book]] buffs, [[Enchantments]] (including [[Punch]] and [[Flame]]), [[Crit Damage]] nor [[Toxic Arrow Poison]].
This bow cannot be [[Enchantments|enchanted]] with [[Dragon Tracer]] or [[Piercing]].
### Upgrading
The Spider Queen's Stinger can be upgraded into a more powerful version. This will add 240 [[Damage]] to it, totaling +600 [[Damage]] and buff its ability to deal 600+(6×Strength) [[True Damage]]. Upgrading it will add the symbol, like all other upgradable items.
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[Dark Queen's Soul]] [[Spider Queen's Stinger]] 1x Spider Queen's Stinger 60x [[Enchanted Dark Oak Log]] 192x [[Soul String]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Dark Oak Log]] x30 ×2 + [[Soul String]] x64 ×3 + [[Dark Queen's Soul]] + Spider Queen's Stinger → [[Spider Queen's Stinger]]|
## Trivia
- When the bow was first released the recipe did not show the requirement of [[Enchanted Dark Oak Log]], making it "unobtainable" for several hours.
- This bow, unlike most others, is capable of harming [[Enderman|Endermen]].
- This weapon is the only weapon that deals [[True Damage]] to mobs, and can be used by players.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Nov 7, 2022|Doubled [[Damage]] dealt by the Spider Queen's Stinger's special effect.|
|Jun 24, 2026|Added a custom texture for Spider Queen's Stinger as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Mar 9, 2021|0.11.2|Added Spider Queen's Stinger.|
|Unknown Date|+1.5 blocks aura radius (from 1 to 2.5).|
|Nov 9, 2022|0.16.1|Doubled [[Damage]] dealt by the Spider Queen's Stinger's special effect.|
|Jul 8, 2026|0.26|Added a custom texture for Spider Queen's Stinger as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|

- The Spiked Atrocity was added as part of 7[[Ranks|[YOUTUBE]]] ThirtyVirus's minister perk during [[Resistance Movement|The Uprising]].
- The Spiked Atrocity's appearance is modeled after the Marvel Universe's Thanos.
  - ThirtyVirus's channel has a longstanding joke related to the [[Spiked Bait]]'s apparent resemblance to Thanos, dubbing the item "Thanofish".

Item ID: BOSS_SPIRIT_BOW
Tooltip: An extremely fragile but powerful bow, capable of damaging Thorn, despite him being a spirit!
The Spirit Bow is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Bows|Bow]] that can be obtained from [[The Catacombs - Floor IV]]. It is the only weapon that can damage [[Thorn]]. The Bow cannot be taken out of [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]].
## Obtaining
The Spirit Bow can be obtained from killing the [[Spirit Bear]] in the [[The Catacombs - Floor IV]], the Spirit Bow is placed in the player's 9th hotbar slot. It only has one shot.
## Usage
Bow:
Spirit Bow
[Effects]
[An extremely fragile but powerful bow, capable of damaging Thorn, despite him being a spirit!]
The Spirit Bow deals 1 hit against [[Thorn]], and is the only weapon that can be used to damage him in the [[The Catacombs - Floor IV]].
## Trivia
- The bow was used to be able to be taken out of the dungeon, using various methods. However, this is not possible anymore[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)].
  - If the bow was shot outside of [[Dungeons]], everyone in the player's lobby would be notified with a message on their screen.
    - This was fixed in a later update.
- This bow, if sold at the [[Auction House]] can not be found by [[Rarity]].

Type: Reforge Stone ([[Reforge Stones]])

Item ID: SPIRIT_DECOY
Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Spiritual reforge when combined with a Bow.; As light as a feather, and you can almost see through it. Some people say looking at it for too long can change your personality.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Strength  Crit Chance  Crit Damage; Spiritual Bonus Killing an enemy has a 10% chance to spawn a Spirit Decoy while in  Dungeons.; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; ❣ Requires Mining Skill 24.; EPIC REFORGE STONE
The Spirit Stone is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] which can be used to apply the [[Reforging|Spiritual]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to [[Bows]], which grants [[Strength]], [[Crit Chance]], and [[Crit Damage]].
## Obtaining
### Boss Collection
The Spirit Stone can be obtained from [[Thorn|Thorn I (1)]], after killing [[Thorn]] 50 times.
### Dungeon Reward Chests
The Spirit Stone can be obtained from [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Dungeon Reward Chests]] in [[The Catacombs - Floor IV]].
|Catacombs Floor ([[Catacombs]])|Chest ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|Cost; (Chest + Added)|Average Chance; (No Bonuses)|Average Chance; (S+ (Score above 300 points.) ([[Dungeon Score]]) Max Bonuses ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]]))|Quality ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|Weight ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Floor IV ([[The Catacombs - Floor IV]])|Diamond|750,000 [[Coins]]|1.0363% (1 in 96.5 runs)|1.8778% (1 in 53.3 runs)|185|2|
|Floor IV ([[The Catacombs - Floor IV]])|Emerald|1,000,000 [[Coins]]|1.8183% (1 in 55 runs)|3.6154% (1 in 27.7 runs)|185|2|
|Floor IV ([[The Catacombs - Floor IV]])|Obsidian|1,500,000 [[Coins]]|4.7449% (1 in 21.1 runs)|6.6786% (1 in 15 runs)|185|2|
|Floor IV ([[The Catacombs - Floor IV]]) (Master Mode)|Diamond|750,000 [[Coins]]|1.0363% (1 in 96.5 runs)|2.1474% (1 in 46.6 runs)|185|2|
|Floor IV ([[The Catacombs - Floor IV]]) (Master Mode)|Emerald|1,000,000 [[Coins]]|2.0788% (1 in 48.1 runs)|3.7726% (1 in 26.5 runs)|185|2|
|Floor IV ([[The Catacombs - Floor IV]]) (Master Mode)|Obsidian|1,500,000 [[Coins]]|5.2533% (1 in 19 runs)|7.1466% (1 in 14 runs)|185|8|
## Usage
### Reforging
Spirit Stone can be applied to [[Bows]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Spiritual]] reforge, to grant [[Strength]], [[Crit Chance]] and [[Crit Damage]], which scale with the bow's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining XXIV (24)]]. A bow that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Spiritual]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.
Additionally, it grants the Spiritual Bonus, which grants 10% chance to spawn a [[Decoy]] after killing an enemy in the [[Dungeons]].
|Stat Bonus|Rarity COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity RARE ([[Rarity]])|Rarity EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Rarity MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity DIVINE ([[Rarity]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Stat Bonus|+4 Str ([[Strength]]) +7 CC ([[Crit Chance]]) +10 CD ([[Crit Damage]])|+8 Str ([[Strength]]) +8 CC ([[Crit Chance]]) +15 CD ([[Crit Damage]])|+14 Str ([[Strength]]) +9 CC ([[Crit Chance]]) +23 CD ([[Crit Damage]])|+20 Str ([[Strength]]) +10 CC ([[Crit Chance]]) +37 CD ([[Crit Damage]])|+28 Str ([[Strength]]) +12 CC ([[Crit Chance]]) +55 CD ([[Crit Damage]])|+38 Str ([[Strength]]) +14 CC ([[Crit Chance]]) +75 CD ([[Crit Damage]])|+50 Str ([[Strength]]) +16 CC ([[Crit Chance]]) +100 CD ([[Crit Damage]])|
|Reforge Bonus|Spiritual Bonus; Killing an enemy has a 10% chance to spawn a Spirit Decoy while in Dungeons.|Spiritual Bonus; Killing an enemy has a 10% chance to spawn a Spirit Decoy while in Dungeons.|Spiritual Bonus; Killing an enemy has a 10% chance to spawn a Spirit Decoy while in Dungeons.|Spiritual Bonus; Killing an enemy has a 10% chance to spawn a Spirit Decoy while in Dungeons.|Spiritual Bonus; Killing an enemy has a 10% chance to spawn a Spirit Decoy while in Dungeons.|Spiritual Bonus; Killing an enemy has a 10% chance to spawn a Spirit Decoy while in Dungeons.|Spiritual Bonus; Killing an enemy has a 10% chance to spawn a Spirit Decoy while in Dungeons.|
|Cost to apply|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|
## Tips
- [[Reforging|Spiritual]] is generally considered the best reforge for [[Bows]].
  - In [[The Catacombs - Floor IV|Master Mode Floor 4]], it should be avoided, since it interferes with the [[Decoy]] that is placed at the beginning of the boss fight to lure [[Spirit Bat|Spirit Bats]].
  - Players with multiple [[Terminator]] should use [[Reforging|Precise]] on the Terminator with [[Soul Eater]] and [[Reforging|Spiritual]] on the Terminator with [[Duplex]].
  - Players in need of [[Crit Chance]] can use [[Reforging|Hasty]].
## Trivia
- The Spirit Decoy's AI similar as a [[Decoy]]. It runs to the nearest mob, tries to damage it and takes aggro of the nearby mobs.
- The Spirit Decoy counts toward the maximum Decoy limit. Therefore, despite obtaining from [[The Catacombs - Floor IV]], this reforge is not good for its Boss Fight, as Decoys may be needed to distract [[Thorn|Spirit Bats]].
