## Trivia
- Sponge Boots used to be named "Spongy Shoes", as opposed to the rest of the set, which followed the usual naming pattern.
- Wearing the full set will unlock the [[Achievements|"Absorb it all!" Achievement]].
- There used to be a bug where a player who wore [[Sponge Armor]] 1 Sponge Armor or [[Shark Scale Armor]] in water and then switched to another armor set while still in the water would gain the 2x [[Defense]] buff, even if they were not wearing Sponge Armor or Shark Scale Armor.
  - This has been fixed in an update.

Item ID: SQUEAKY_TOY
Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Squeaky reforge when combined with Equipment.; So lifelike!; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Bonus Pest Chance  Farming Fortune; Squeaky Bonus  Pests spawn 2.5% faster.; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; ❣ Requires Mining Skill 21.; EPIC REFORGE STONE
A Squeaky Toy is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] which can apply the [[Reforging|Squeaky]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to [[Equipment]], which grants [[Bonus Pest Chance]] and [[Farming Fortune]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv1]|[[Field Mouse]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|5%* ([[Pests]])|
## Usage
### Reforging
Squeaky Toy can be applied to [[Equipment]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Squeaky]] reforge, to grant [[Bonus Pest Chance]] and [[Farming Fortune]], which scale with the equipment's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining XXI (21)]] and can be done with any equipment, regardless of the piece. Equipment that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Squeaky]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.
Additionally, it grants the Squeaky Bonus, which makes [[Pests (Currency)|Pests]] spawn 2.5% faster.
|Stat Bonus|Rarity COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity RARE ([[Rarity]])|Rarity EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Rarity MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity DIVINE ([[Rarity]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Stat Bonus|+2 Frm.Frt ([[Farming Fortune]]) +0.5 BPC ([[Bonus Pest Chance]])|+4 Frm.Frt ([[Farming Fortune]]) +0.5 BPC ([[Bonus Pest Chance]])|+6 Frm.Frt ([[Farming Fortune]]) +1 BPC ([[Bonus Pest Chance]])|+8 Frm.Frt ([[Farming Fortune]]) +1.5 BPC ([[Bonus Pest Chance]])|+10 Frm.Frt ([[Farming Fortune]]) +2 BPC ([[Bonus Pest Chance]])|+12 Frm.Frt ([[Farming Fortune]]) +2.5 BPC ([[Bonus Pest Chance]])|+15 Frm.Frt ([[Farming Fortune]]) +3 BPC ([[Bonus Pest Chance]])|
|Reforge Bonus|Squeaky Bonus; Pests spawn 2.5% faster.|Squeaky Bonus; Pests spawn 2.5% faster.|Squeaky Bonus; Pests spawn 2.5% faster.|Squeaky Bonus; Pests spawn 2.5% faster.|Squeaky Bonus; Pests spawn 2.5% faster.|Squeaky Bonus; Pests spawn 2.5% faster.|Squeaky Bonus; Pests spawn 2.5% faster.|
|Cost to apply|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|
### Pesthunter Phillip
[[Squeaky Toy]] 1 Squeaky Toy can be used to obtain [[Pesthunter Phillip]]'s [[Abiphones|Abiphone Contact]].
## Tips
- It is recommended to apply the [[Reforging|Squeaky]] reforge to the [[Pesthunter's Set|Pesthunter Set]] and [[Pest Vest]], due to their high [[Bonus Pest Chance]] and Pest cooldown reduction. However, it is better to apply [[Reforging|Rooted]] or [[Reforging|Thorny]] to pieces of the [[Blossom Set]] instead.

# Startlevine
Categories: Epic items, Items in Sacks, Items, Mutations
[Startlevine]
Type: Mutation
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Small Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Medium Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Large Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: STARTLEVINE
Tooltip: This skittish plant bolts the moment you get too close, disappearing into the ground. You'll need to figure out how to get near without scaring it off.; Analyze this Mutation at the Crop Analyzer in Jake's Underground Lab.; EPIC MUTATION
A Startlevine is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Mutations|Mutation]].
## Obtaining
A Startlevine can grow in the [[Greenhouse]] if there are 4 [[Blastberry]] and 4 [[Cheesebite]] within 1 block of farmland.
While growing, a Startlevine needs water and has 12 Growth Stages. When placed near another crop, it retains watering status by 100%, increases [[Farming]] experience by 30% and reduces yield by -20%.
Startlevine requires [[Invisibility Potion|Invisibility]] to be harvested.
## Crop Drops
When harvested, Startlevines also drop 2,250 (35 stacks plus 10) [[Cactus]], 3,000 (46 stacks plus 56) [[Sugar Cane]], and +12,000 [[Farming|Farming XP]].
## Usage
### Crop Analyzer
A Startlevine can be analyzed in the [[Crop Analyzer]] for 500,000 [[Coins]]. When analyzed, it grants 250 [[Copper]]. The first time analyzing a Startlevine grants an additional 1,000 [[Copper]].
### Mutations
Startlevines are used to create a [[Glasscorn]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Approaching without Invisibility ([[Invisibility Potion]])|[CROP] Startlevine: Eeek!|
|Attempting to harvest without Invisibility ([[Invisibility Potion]])|[CROP] Startlevine: I'm too scared to be harvested right now...|
## Gallery
- Startlevine in the Greenhouse Startlevine in the [[Greenhouse]]
## Tips
- A Startlevine can be harvested without losing [[Farming Fortune]] by consuming or getting splashed with an [[Invisibility Potion]], or gaining temporary invisibility through the [[Great Spook Staff]]'s Spook ability.
  - If [[Farming Fortune]] is not a concern, [[Sorrow Armor]] and [[Shadow Assassin Armor|Shadow Assassin Helmet]]'s ability can also be used.
- Since [[Blastberry]] is required to grow Startlevine, any Startlevine actively growing will be destroyed if the [[Blastberry]] decays.
## History

|Dec 5, 2025|Added Startlevine.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Dec 15, 2025|0.24|Added Startlevine.|

# Stereo Pants
Aliases: Stereo, Stereo Leggings
Categories: Epic items, Armor, Craftable items, Sponge Collection, Leggings
[Stereo Pants]
Type: [[Leggings]]
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
Collection ([[Collections]]): Sponge VII (7) ([[Sponge]])
Tooltip Text: Plays sweet tunes that you and people nearby can hear!
Stats: [[Defense]]: +35; [[Treasure Chance]]: +1
[Item Properties]
Enchantable: ✔ Yes
Reforgeable: ✔ Yes
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes

Color: ⁠ #04CFD3
[Shop]
Sell: 1,000 [[Coins]]
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 120x (1 stack plus 56 (120 in total)) [[Sponge]] 4x [[Prismarine Shard]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: MUSIC_PANTS
Tooltip: Defense: +35; Treasure Chance: +1%; Plays sweet tunes that you and nearby players can hear!; Ability: Beat Saver While worn, grants a small chance to catch Music Discs while fishing.; This item can be reforged!; EPIC LEGGINGS
Stereo Pants are an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Armor]] piece unlocked at [[Sponge|Sponge VII (7)]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Sponge VII (7) ([[Sponge]])|3x [[Enchanted Sponge]] 4x [[Prismarine Shard]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Sponge]] ×3 + [[Prismarine Shard]] ×4 → Stereo Pants|
## Usage
Leggings:
Stereo Pants [[Defense]]: +35 [[Treasure Chance]]: +1%
[Effects]
[Ability: Beat Saver; While worn, grants a small chance to catch Music Discs while fishing.]
Stereo Pants give +1 [[Treasure Chance]] as well as a 3% chance to replace any catch (including [[Sea Creatures]]) with vanilla [[Vanilla Objects/Music Discs|Music Disc]].
When worn, Stereo Pants also play music through [[Noteblock|Note Block]] sound effects. The song played is selected at random. To change the song or reactivate the pants, the player needs to take off the pants and then put them back on, or switch lobbies.
## Game Messages
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|If standing near spawnpoint|Your Stereo Pants will start playing when you leave spawn!|
|Fishing up a music disk|♪ MUSICAL CATCH! You caught a Music Disc - [disc name]!|
## Songs
- Nyny - A cover of New York, New York by Frank Sinatra, but at a faster tempo.
- Pump it Up - An original composition made by Hyperdron for the 2015 note block contest, originally called Pumped Up!.
- Flowering Nights - A cover of Sakuya's battle theme of the same name from Touhou 9: Phantasmagoria of Flower View.
- Chased by Creeper - A cover of Fly Above Hatoyama at Night, from Perfect Memento in Strict Sense, an album complementing a Touhou Project book of the same name.
- Gamemode8 - A cover of Leaf Ponders the Flower, Flower Ponders the Leaf, the theme of a fan-made stage in Touhou 11: Subterranean Animism.
- Original - An original composition made by MusicByPedro, first used as the pregame lobby theme of Turbo Kart Racers.
- Red House - A cover of Magician's Melancholy.
- Moonrvg – Build Battle OST
- Walking Mega – Build Battle OST
## Trivia
- The songs played on the Stereo Pants are also available in housing for 2[[Ranks|[VIP]]] and above.
- The Stereo Pants are a reference to the Hypixel Pit item, which has the same songs.
- The ability name Beat Saver could be a reference to the VR video game Beat Saber.
- For treasure catches, if you would normally get 16.16 [[Treasure Chance]] or higher without it, the additional bonus from Stereo Pants will not grant more treasure catches compared to [[Abyssal Armor|Abyssal Leggings]] when accounting for the odds of fishing up a music disc instead.
## Bugs
- Music discs caught via this item's ability does not get deleted by [[Personal Deletor 4000|Personal Deletors]]
## History

|Mar 12, 2025|Added +1 [[Treasure Chance]].|
|Mar 12, 2025|Added Beat Saver ability.|
|Jan 20, 2026|0.24.1 RC|Stereo Pants music discs no longer count as a treasure catch, and now give a custom message when fished up instead.|

# Stock of Stonks
Aliases: Stock Of Stonks, Stocks of Stonks, Stonk of Stonks, Stonks
Categories: Epic items, Items
[Stock of Stonks]
Type: Item
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: STOCK_OF_STONKS
Tooltip: Can be traded in to Liz when Diaz is Mayor for special items!; Started as a shady venture by an estranged comrade of Diaz's. She later adopted it herself for personal use to fuel her candidacy.; EPIC
The Stock of Stonks is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] item.
## Obtaining
Stock of Stonks can be obtained in 3 ways:
- 1 from the [[Relic of Coins]] with a probability of 0.002472% (1 in 40,444 rolls), every 60 seconds
- If [[Diaz]] is elected with her Stock Exchange perk:
  - 10 from winning an item in [[Shen's Auction]];
  - 1 → 25 from [[Richard]]'s [[Richard|Stonks Auction]], depending on the amount of [[Coins]] bid and placement relative to other players, with a minimum of 2,000,000 [[Coins]].
## Usage
Stock of Stonks can be used to purchase various items from [[Liz]] in the [[Trade Center]].
|Item|Cost|Notes|
|-|-|-|
|[[Freshly-Minted Coins]]|[[Stock of Stonks]] 1 Stock of Stonks|This item has a SkyBlock-wide limit of 5,000 units every 4h 0m.|
|[[Golden Fragment]]|[[Stock of Stonks]] 3 Stock of Stonks||
|[[Golden Bounty]]|[[Stock of Stonks]] 20 Stock of Stonks||
|[[Avaricious Chalice]]|[[Stock of Stonks]] 50 Stock of Stonks||
3 of them can be combined through [[Zarina]]'s [[Zarina|Midas Anvil]] to upgrade the [[Coins]] paid on [[Midas' Sword]] or [[Midas Staff]], alongside the [[Coins]] difference.
## Trivia
- Excluding [[Golden Bounty]], utilizing all of [[Liz]]'s products to max potential requires [[Stock of Stonks]] 338 (5 stacks plus 18) Stocks of Stonk.
  - [[Stock of Stonks]] 116 (1 stack plus 52) Stocks of Stonk are required to get [[Freshly-Minted Coins]] for both [[Relic of Coins]] and [[Scavenger Artifact]].
  - [[Stock of Stonks]] 72 (1 stack plus 8) Stocks of Stonk are required to upgrade all items using [[Golden Fragment|Golden Fragments]].
  - [[Stock of Stonks]] 150 (2 stacks plus 22) Stocks of Stonk are required for both [[Crown of Avarice]] and [[Emerald Artifact]].
- The original lore of this item had a warning that stated:
  - WARNING: RISKY INVESTMENT This is a TEST item, may get deleted!
- The Stock of Stonks was originally sold by [[Warren]] for 200 [[Coins]]. This item was intended to be valueless, to be used as a token simply to test if the Bazaar was working as intended.
  - Less than a day after its debut, Warren stopped selling Stocks, because enough had been purchased to test the Bazaar comfortably. Players quickly listed the Stock on the Bazaar, and its price fluctuated wildly until it settled somewhere above its original cost price.
  - During this time, Warren's GUI indicated that players would, one day, be able to sell Stocks back to Warren.
  - Warren was removed with the update on May 19, 2020, with the line in the changelog reading: "Warren is going on vacation for a long long time."
  - Over time, the Stock's original purpose was forgotten, and players took time to think about the description on the item. Tempted by the potential of 100x riches, some players have hoards of Stocks ready to be turned into something else, or sold back on the Bazaar for, already, over 43000x its original value.
## History

|Jun 3, 2024|Stock of Stonks can now be used to purchase items from [[Liz]].|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Mar 9, 2020|0.7.7|Added Stock of Stonks.|
|Mar 9, 2020|0.7.7|Stock of Stonks become unobtainable within 24 hours.|
|Jul 2, 2024|0.20.3|Stock of Stonks are now obtainable again.|

The Stonk is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Pickaxes|Pickaxe]] bought from the [[Pearl Dealer]].
## Obtaining
A Stonk can be purchased from the [[Pearl Dealer]]'s shop in [[The End]] for 499,999 [[Coins]]. It comes pre-enchanted with [[Efficiency|Efficiency VI]].
## Usage
Pickaxe:
Stonk [[Breaking Power]]: +1 [[Mining Speed]]: +380
[Effects]
[Efficiency VI (Efficiency); Increases how quickly your tool breaks blocks.; When mining End Stone with this Pickaxe, Nest Endermites (Nest Endermite) won't spawn, and Experience will be dropped. 5% chance to drop an extra End Stone block.]
## Trivia
- The name of the item is a reference to the Stonks meme.
- Outside of areas affected by the custom mining speed mechanics, it was able to instantly break certain blocks, due to [[Efficiency|Efficiency VI]].
  - This allowed the player to pass through 1-block thick walls and stairs, as well as create ghost blocks with high enough [[Speed]].
  - This mechanic was however replaced with [[Dungeonbreaker]], for variety of reasons explained here.
- Stonk can be used to avoid spawning [[Nest Endermite|Nest Endermites]] while breaking [[Ender Nodes]].

# Stoplight Petal
Aliases: Stoplight
Categories: Epic items, Items in Sacks, Items, Mutations
[Stoplight Petal]
Type: Mutation
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Small Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Medium Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Large Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: STOPLIGHT_PETAL
Tooltip: This flower switches between green and red as you try to harvest it.; Break it when it's green, but don't touch it when it turns red or it'll wither!; Analyze this Mutation at the Crop Analyzer in Jake's Underground Lab.; EPIC MUTATION
A Stoplight Petal is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Mutations|Mutation]].
## Obtaining
A Stoplight Petal can grow in the [[Greenhouse]] if there are 4 [[Snoozling]] and 4 [[Noctilume]] within 1 block of farmland.
While growing, a Stoplight Petal needs water and has 12 Growth Stages. When placed near another crop, it retains watering status by 100%, spreads buffs and debuffs to adjacent crops, and reduces yield by -20%.
When harvested, Stoplight Petal switches between green and red. The player has to repeatedly punch it while green to fill up a progress bar. While punching, the Stoplight Petal can become yellow to signal it soon turning red. If the Stoplight Petal is punched while red, the progress bar is reset and the player may try again immediatly with no penalty. It remains green for 5 seconds, yellow for 1 second, and red for 2 seconds. Once it turns green again, the cycle repeats until harvested or the Stoplight Petal is punched while red.
## Crop Drops
When harvested, Stoplight Petals also drop 7,500 (117 stacks plus 12) [[Cactus]], 10,000 (156 stacks plus 16) [[Wild Rose]], and +40,000 [[Farming|Farming XP]].
## Usage
### Crop Analyzer
A Stoplight Petal can be analyzed in the [[Crop Analyzer]] for 4,000,000 [[Coins]]. When analyzed, it grants 2,000 [[Copper]]. The first time analyzing a Stoplight Petal grants an additional 1,250 [[Copper]].
### Mutations
Stoplight Petals are used to create a [[Timestalk]].
## Trivia
The Stoplight Petal is the only [[Mutations|Mutation]] used to make other crops with no Mutation Value.
## Gallery
- Stoplight Petal in the Greenhouse Stoplight Petal in the [[Greenhouse]]
- Stoplight Petal during its minigame

A Summoning Eye is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] item. It is primarily used for summoning the [[Ender Dragon]] in the [[Dragon's Nest]].
## Obtaining
### Special Zealot
When a player kills a [[Zealot]] or [[Zealot Bruiser]], there is a small chance that a [[Special Zealot]] will spawn nearby: this is telegraphed by a message displaying SPECIAL ZEALOT on their screen. Additionally, the following dialogue will display in chat:
A special Zealot has spawned nearby!
[[Special Zealot|Special Zealots]] have a [[Drop Chance|1Guaranteed]] chance to drop [[Summoning Eye]] 1 Summoning Eye.
The [[Tyzzo|Dragon Piper]] perk and [[Attributes|Special Upgrades]] [[Attributes|Attribute]] grant a +5% and +0.5-5% chance to be [[Recombobulator 3000|recombobulated]], up to a 10% chance. Upon one of those activating, the following dialogue appears in chat:
DRAGON PIPER! Your Summoning Eye has been recombobulated!
SPECIAL UPGRADES! Your Summoning Eye has been recombobulated!
### Voidgloom Seraph
A Summoning Eye is an [[Drop Chance|7Extraordinary]] drop obtained from [[Voidgloom Seraph]] Tier II+ if the player has unlocked [[Enderman Slayer|Enderman Slayer I (1)]].
Drop Chances:
- Tier II: 0.67% (1 in 148.5)
- Tier III: 0.6% (1 in 167.75)
- Tier IV: 0.56% (1 in 177.11)
### Voidling Extremist
A Summoning Eye is a [[Drop Chance|8Legendary Loot]] drop obtained from [[Voidling Extremist]] with 0.04% chance.
### Draconic Altar
A Summoning Eye has a Very Rare chance to be obtained by sacrificing the following items at the [[Draconic Altar]]:
- Any [[Dragon Armor]]: 4.82%
- [[Ender Armor]] and Equipment: 1%
- [[End Stone Bow]]: 1%
- [[Obsidian Chestplate]]: 1.05%
- [[Aspect of the Dragons]]: 4.71%
### Raffle of the Century
Summoning Eye can also be obtained as a MINOR reward from [[Raffle of the Century]], or from the [[Gift Bag of the Century]].
## Usage
### Ender Altar
Summoning Eyes are used at the [[Dragon's Nest|Ender Altar]] to summon a [[Ender Dragon]], of which there are 7 types. Interacting with an empty End Portal Frame will place the Summoning Eye and convert it to a [[Sleeping Eye]]. A total of [[Summoning Eye]] 8 Summoning Eyes are required to summon an [[Ender Dragon]]: once an [[Ender Dragon]] is summoned, all [[Sleeping Eye|Sleeping Eyes]] are converted to [[Remnant of the Eye|Remnants of the Eye]].
Placing Recombobulated [[Summoning Eye]] Summoning Eyes grant a greater chance to spawn a [[Ender Dragon|Superior Dragon]].
|Recomboblated; Summoning Eyes|Spawn Chance|
|-|-|
|0x|4%|
|1x|12.5%|
|2x|25%|
|3x|37.5%|
|4x|50%|
|5x|62.5%|
|6x|75%|
|7x|87.5%|
|8x|100%|
### Kat
[[Summoning Eye]] 8 Summoning Eye and 400M [[Coins]] are used to upgrade the [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Ender Dragon Pet]] to [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Soul Esoward]]|Enderman Slayer III (3) ([[Enderman Slayer]])|[[Summoning Eye]] 1x Summoning Eye 13x [[Null Ovoid]] 48x [[Enchanted Birch Log]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Birch Log]] x18 + Summoning Eye + [[Null Ovoid]] x4 + [[Null Ovoid]] x6 + [[Enchanted Birch Log]] x14 + [[Enchanted Birch Log]] x5 + [[Null Ovoid]] + [[Enchanted Birch Log]] x11 + [[Null Ovoid]] x2 → [[Soul Esoward]]|
|[[Obsidian Tablet]]|Obsidian IX (9) ([[Obsidian]])|[[Summoning Eye]] 1x Summoning Eye 192x (3 stacks (192 items total)) [[Enchanted Obsidian]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Obsidian]] x24 ×8 + Summoning Eye → [[Obsidian Tablet]]|
|Medium Dragon Sack ([[Dragon Sack]])|Ender Pearl VI (6) ([[Ender Pearl]])|1x Small Dragon Sack ([[Dragon Sack]]) [[Summoning Eye]] 4x Summoning Eye 16x [[Crystal Fragment]]|Recipe: [[Crystal Fragment]] x4 ×4 + Summoning Eye ×4 + [[Dragon Sack]] → [[Dragon Sack]]|
### Stranded
This content is exclusive to the [[Stranded]] gamemode.
A Summoning Eye can be used as a minion upgrade for the [[Enderman Minion]], allowing it to spawn [[Zealot|Zealots]] instead, at the cost of a 2x reduction in the minion's speed.
## Trivia
- The addition of guaranteeing a [[Ender Dragon|Superior Dragon]] from applying [[Recombobulator 3000|Recombobulator 3000s]] to Summoning Eyes is a reference to 7[[Ranks|[YOUTUBE]]] Refraction, who recombobulated Summoning Eyes in the hopes of getting a Superior Dragon.
- Prior to v0.11.4, there was a 5s pickup priority before a player can take a different player's [[Special Zealot]]'s Summoning Eye.

A Sundial is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] item.
## Usage
A Sundial can be given to [[Sam]] to permanently unlock Crop Speed settings in [[The Garden|The Desk]], which allows the player to change their maximum [[Speed]] while harvesting individual crops.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Sunflower VII (7) ([[Sunflower]])|5x [[Chloronite]] 16x [[Compacted Sunflower]]|Recipe: [[Compacted Sunflower]] x2 ×8 + [[Chloronite]] x5 → Sundial|
## Trivia
- Upon gifting a Sundial to [[Sam]], a physical Sundial appears in [[The Garden]] near the player's barn. Interacting with it displays the following message: The sundial reads <time>.
- Breaking tall grass in a garden plot will trigger the set speed cap for [[Sunflower|Sunflowers]]/[[Moonflower|Moonflowers]]. This likely happens because they all share the same base block id in older versions of Minecraft, that being double_plant.

### Minion Upgrade
Super Jacob Entertainment System can be placed in farming [[Minions]], see below. It is limited to one minion per profile at a time. It generates 1 [[Jacob's Ticket]] every 3h, granting per hour or every day when sold on Bazaar or 80,000 [[Coins]] to [[Shops]].

- The Super Jacob Entertainment System references the Super Nintendo Entertainment System console.

- This item functions as a replacement of [[Jacob's Participation Medal]], due to the latter's design being undesirable.

Sword:

If the below [[Mob|Mobs]] reached the [[Health]] thresholds or below, the Super Sharp 'n Stabby Steak Stake can deal +1000 [[Rift Damage]] to the mob.
|Vampiric Mob|Threshold|
|-|-|
|[[Fledgling]]|42 [[Health]]|
|[[Thrall]]|38 [[Health]]|
|[[Splatter]]|32 [[Health]]|
|[[Riftstalker Bloodfiend]] I|125 [[Health]]|
|[[Riftstalker Bloodfiend]] II|220 [[Health]]|

## Tips
- Using the [[Super Sharp 'n Stabby Steak Stake]] Super Sharp 'n Stabby Steak Stake allows to reduce the kill requirement to spawn the [[Riftstalker Bloodfiend]] from 10/15/20/25/30 kills per tier to 7/10/14/17/20.
  - With a [[Perma-Jelled Garlic-Flavored Re-Heated Gummy Polar Bear]], this can be further reduced to 7/10/13/16/19.
## Trivia
- The Bloodcrave ability's [[Damage]] bonus only applies to hits from the Super Sharp 'n Stabby Steak Stake itself.
  - It applies even if another hit with a different weapon was performed in meantime.
- If the player deals enough damage to kill a Vampire above 20% [[Health]], it does not trigger the Bloodcrave ability.

Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 666,176x (10,409 stacks (666,176 items total)) [[Carrot]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SUPERB_CARROT_CANDY
Tooltip: Consumable; Carrot Candy gives pets boosts of Pet EXP!; Pets can only eat 10 candy in their lifetime!; Grants 750,000 Pet EXP.; Right-click on your summoned pet to feed it!; EPIC
Superb Carrot Candy is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] item that grants a [[Pets|Pet]] an instant [[Pets|XP]] boost of 750,000. Each pet can only use up to 10x [[Candy (disambiguation)|Carrot Candies]] of any level in their lifetime.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Carrot IX (9) ([[Carrot]])|1x [[Great Carrot Candy]] 24x [[Enchanted Golden Carrot]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Golden Carrot]] x3 ×8 + [[Great Carrot Candy]] → Superb Carrot Candy|
## Usage
Superb Carrot Candy can be consumed onto a [[Pets|Pet]] to instantly grant it 750,000 [[Pets|XP]]. Each pet can only use up to 10x [[Candy (disambiguation)|Carrot Candies]] of any level in their lifetime.
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Ultimate Carrot Candy]]|None|1x [[Ultimate Carrot Candy Upgrade]] [[Superb Carrot Candy]] 8x Superb Carrot Candy|Recipe: Superb Carrot Candy ×8 + [[Ultimate Carrot Candy Upgrade]] → [[Ultimate Carrot Candy]] x10|
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|May 5, 2020|0.7.8|Added Superb Carrot Candy.|
|Mar 24, 2023|Fixed a typo in the Superb Carrot Candy's lore.|
|Apr 18, 2023|0.18.3|Added Superb Carrot Candy to the [[Bazaar]].|
|Jan 16, 2024|0.19.10|Superb Carrot Candy now gives 750,000 XP instead of 500,000.|

# Supreme Voter's Badge
Categories: Epic items, Accessories
[Supreme Voter's Badge]
Type: Accessory ([[Accessories]])
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
Source: Mayor ([[Mayor Election]]) reward as [[Garden Visitors]]
[Upgrades]
← Previous: [[Elite Voter's Badge]]
Next →: None
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✕ No; (Except to Co-op members)
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Soulbound: ✔ Yes (Co-op)
[Shop]
Sell: 5,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: VOTER_BADGE_SUPREME
Tooltip: Accessory Power: +12; Grants +12 votes in Mayor Elections.; * Co-op Soulbound * Works while in Accessory Bag!; EPIC ACCESSORY
Supreme Voter's Badge is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Accessories|Accessory]].
## Obtaining
While a mayoral candidate is elected as [[Mayor Election|Mayor]], they have a chance to appear as a [[Garden Visitors|Garden Visitor]]. A Supreme Voter's Badge will be rewarded to the player after accepting the offer of 8 unique mayor visitors.
If lost, the Supreme Voter's Badge can be repurchased at [[Rusty]] for 75,000 [[Coins]].
## Usage
The Supreme Voter's Badge grants +12 votes in [[Mayor Election|Mayor Elections]] while in the player's [[Accessory Bag]].
## Trivia
- The Supreme Voter's Badge cannot have its rarity upgraded using a [[Recombobulator 3000]].
- Voter's Badge progress is on a per-player basis, meaning a player accepting a mayor visitor will not count towards their co-op members' progress.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Dec 5, 2025|Added Supreme Voter's Badge.|
|Jan 26, 2026|Fixed all mayor visitors counting towards Voter's Badge accessory line progress instead of only unique ones.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Dec 15, 2025|0.24|Added Supreme Voter's Badge.|
|Jan 27, 2026|0.24.1|Fixed all mayor visitors counting towards Voter's Badge accessory line progress instead of only unique ones.|

# Tactical Insertion
Categories: Epic items, Items, Craftable items, Slayer, Blaze Slayer
[Tactical Insertion]
Type: Item
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
Slayer Level ([[Slayer]]) Requirement: Blaze Slayer V (5) ([[Blaze Slayer]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes (+14 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]]))
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 512,000x (8,000 stacks (512,000 items total)) [[Ender Pearl]] 3,276,800x (51,200 stacks (3,276,800 items total)) [[Lapis Lazuli]] 6,144x (96 stacks (6,144 items total)) [[Derelict Ashe]] 30,720x (480 stacks (30,720 items total)) [[Red Sand]] 30,720x [[Mycelium]] 122,880x (1,920 stacks (122,880 items total)) [[Magma Cream]] 122,880x [[Glowstone Dust]] 122,880x [[Nether Wart]] 122,880x [[Blaze Rod]] 8x [[Inferno Vertex]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: TACTICAL_INSERTION
Tooltip: Ability: Gorilla Tactics RIGHT CLICK Marks your location and teleport back there after 3s.; On coming back, burn enemies within 3 blocks and set your  Health to HALF of what it was.; The burn deals 10% of ALL damage you dealt within the 3s, spreading over 6s.; Mana Cost: 180 Cooldown: 20s; ☠ Requires Blaze Slayer 5.; EPIC
The Tactical Insertion is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] item. It is unlocked at [[Blaze Slayer|Blaze Slayer V (5)]]
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Blaze Slayer V (5) ([[Blaze Slayer]])|4x [[Tessellated Ender Pearl]] 8x [[Inferno Vertex]] 48x [[Molten Powder]]|Recipe: [[Tessellated Ender Pearl]] x4 + [[Inferno Vertex]] x8 + [[Molten Powder]] x48 → Tactical Insertion|
## Usage
The Tactical Insertion's Gorilla Tactics ability marks the player's locations, teleporting them to the marked location after 3s. Upon teleporting, the Tactical Insertion burns nearby enemies within 3 blocks and sets the player's [[Health]] to half of what it was prior to teleporting. The burn deals 10% of all damage dealt in the last 3s, spreading out over a 6s period. The ability costs 200 [[Mana]] and has a 20s cooldown.
## Trivia
- The Tactical Insertion can deal full damage to all mobs, regardless of any damage reduction.
  - This means it can one shot a Tier II+ [[Inferno Demonlord]], regardless of whether their Hellion Shield is active.
- The ability name Gorilla Tactics is a play on the term Guerrilla warfare, which refers to a situation when a small non-military group faces typically larger military forces.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Mar 3, 2022|5[MVP+] ([[Ranks]]) Minikloon leaks Tactical Insertion.|
|Mar 21, 2022|Added Tactical Insertion.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Apr 20, 2022|0.13|Added Tactical Insertion.|
|Jun 21, 2023|Tactical Insertion can now be donated to the museum.|

|3|1000|+80 [[Defense]]|
|4|2000|+110 [[Defense]]|
|5|3000|+135 [[Defense]]|
|6|5000|+160 [[Defense]]|
|7|7500|+180 [[Defense]]|
|8|10000|+200 [[Defense]]|
|9|15000|+215 [[Defense]]|
|10|25000|+235 [[Defense]]|
|11|50000|+250 [[Defense]]|
|12|100000|+270 [[Defense]]|
|13|200000|+280 [[Defense]]|
|14|500000|+285 [[Defense]]|
Additionally, each piece of Tarantula Armor has a unique piece bonus:
- The [[Tarantula Armor|Tarantula Helmet]]'s Radioactive perk grants +1 [[Crit Damage]] per 10 [[Strength]], up to +100 [[Crit Damage]] at 1000 [[Strength]].
- The [[Tarantula Armor|Tarantula Chestplate]]'s Anti-Toxin perk reduces the effectiveness of [[Tarantula Broodfather]]'s [[Vitality]] reduction by 25%.
- The [[Tarantula Armor|Tarantula Leggings]]' Brood perk increases the base stats of the [[Tarantula Pet]] by 50%.
  - This comes out to 5 [[Strength]], 5 [[Crit Chance]], and 15 [[Crit Damage]] if the [[Tarantula Pet]] is level 100.
- The [[Tarantula Boots]]' Double Jump ability allows the player to double jump in mid-air, costing 40 [[Mana]].

|Primordial Chestplate ([[Primordial Armor]])|Spider Slayer VII (7) ([[Spider Slayer]])|1x Tarantula Chestplate ([[Tarantula Armor]]) 1x [[Tarantula Catalyst]] 6x [[Enchanted Iron Block]] 64x [[Tarantula Silk]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Iron Block]] ×6 + [[Tarantula Catalyst]] + [[Tarantula Armor]] + [[Tarantula Silk]] x64 → [[Primordial Armor]]|
|Primordial Leggings ([[Primordial Armor]])|Spider Slayer VII (7) ([[Spider Slayer]])|1x Tarantula Leggings ([[Tarantula Armor]]) 1x [[Tarantula Catalyst]] 6x [[Enchanted Iron Block]] 64x [[Tarantula Silk]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Iron Block]] ×6 + [[Tarantula Catalyst]] + [[Tarantula Armor]] + [[Tarantula Silk]] x64 → [[Primordial Armor]]|
|Primordial Boots ([[Primordial Armor]])|Spider Slayer VII (7) ([[Spider Slayer]])|1x [[Tarantula Boots]] 1x [[Tarantula Catalyst]] 6x [[Enchanted Iron Block]] 64x [[Tarantula Silk]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Iron Block]] ×6 + [[Tarantula Catalyst]] + [[Tarantula Boots]] + [[Tarantula Silk]] x64 → [[Primordial Armor]]|

## Tips
- The Tarantula Helmet is commonly used in place of other armor sets' helmets, due to Radioactive granting a hefty damage buff for a large section of the game. It is often paired with the armors that give high strength such as [[Shadow Assassin Armor]], [[Necron's Armor]] and [[Crimson Armor]].
## Trivia
- Tarantula Armor is one of 9 armor pieces which have the darkest color, with a hex code of ⁠ #000000. The others are [[Bat Person Armor]], [[Obsidian Chestplate]], [[Squid Boots]], [[Shadow Assassin Armor]], [[Rancher's Boots]], [[Necromancer Lord Armor|Necromancer Lord Chestplate]], [[Snorkeling Armor]] and [[Wither Armor]].

# Tarantula Boots
Categories: Epic items, Armor, Craftable items, Slayer, Spider Slayer
[Tarantula Boots]
Type: [[Armor]]
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
Slayer Level ([[Slayer]]) Requirement: Spider Slayer IV (4) ([[Spider Slayer]])
Stats: [[Health]]: +70; [[Defense]]: +70; [[Intelligence]]: +50; [[Speed]]: +5%
[Upgrades]
← Previous: [[Spider's Boots]]
Next →: Primordial Boots ([[Primordial Armor]])
[Item Properties]

Reforgeable: ✔ Yes
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No

[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 40,960x (640 stacks (40,960 items total)) [[String]] 20,480x (320 stacks (20,480 items total)) [[Flint]] 30,720x (480 stacks (30,720 items total)) [[Iron Ingot]] 512x (8 stacks (512 items total)) [[Tarantula Web]] 1x [[Spider Catalyst]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: TARANTULA_BOOTS
Tooltip: Health: +70; Defense: +70; Intelligence: +50; Speed: +5; Full Set Bonus: Octodexterity (0\ 4); Every 4th strike, deal +100%  Damage and apply Venom reducing healing by 40% for 4 seconds.; Piece Bonus: Spider Bulwark Kill Spiders to accumulate defense against them.; Piece Bonus: +0 Next Upgrade: +20 (0\ 50); Ability: Double Jump DOUBLE TAP JUMP Allows you to double jump mid air!; Mana Cost: 40; This item is Arachnal !; This item can be reforged!; ☠ Requires Spider Slayer 4.; EPIC BOOTS
Tarantula Boots are an [[Rarity|EPIC]] pair of [[Boots]] that are part of the [[Tarantula Armor]] set, and are unlocked through getting [[Spider Slayer|Spider Slayer LVL IV (4)]].
This is one of the few items part of two sets; both the [[Tarantula Armor]] set and the "secret" [[Monster Raider Armor]] set. While other parts of the secret set are technically in two sets, they're both just different versions of the Monster Armor sets, while the Tarantula Boots are in two distinct sets (since the [[Spider's Boots]] are used in the weaker [[Monster Hunter Armor]] set).
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Spider Slayer IV (4) ([[Spider Slayer]])|1x [[Spider Catalyst]] 1x [[Spider's Boots]] 4x [[Tarantula Silk]] 192x (3 stacks (192 items total)) [[Enchanted Iron Ingot]]|Recipe: [[Tarantula Silk]] ×4 + [[Spider Catalyst]] + [[Enchanted Iron Ingot]] x64 ×3 + [[Spider's Boots]] → Tarantula Boots|
## Usage
Boots:
Tarantula Boots [[Health]]: +70 [[Defense]]: +100 [[Intelligence]]: +50 [[Speed]]: +5
[Effects]
[Ability: Double Jump DOUBLE TAP JUMP; Allows you to double jump mid air!; Mana Cost (Intelligence): 40 Piece Bonus: Spider Bulwark; Kill Spiders (Spider) to accumulate Defense against them.]
## Piece Bonus
Similar to [[Revenant Armor]], [[Defense]] against Spiders can be upgraded.
|Tier|Total Spider Kills|Def ([[Defense]]) against Spiders|
|-|-|-|
|1|50|+20|
|2|300|+45|
|3|1000|+80|
|4|2000|+110|
|5|3000|+135|
|6|5000|+160|
|7|7500|+180|
|8|10,000|+200|
|9|15,000|+215|
|10|25,000|+235|
|11|50,000|+250|
|12|100,000|+270|
|13|200,000|+280|
|14|500,000|+285|
## Notes
- Kills do not reset when upgrading.
- Each piece of armor will have its own number of kills.
- The full set is not needed to enable the piece bonus.
## Trivia
- On March 26, 2020, Tarantula Boots in inventories of SkyBlock players vanished when they logged off. Many players were affected and Tarantula Boots temporarily disappeared from the auction house. Admins returned the items to many of the players affected.
- The Double Jump ability, in addition to vertically boosting the player, also propels the player in the direction that they are looking.
## History

[SkyBlock Prototype]

|Nov 8, 2021|Fixed an exploit where the Tarantula Boots could be used in Trap rooms within The Catacombs ([[Catacombs]]).|

|Oct 6, 2026|0.27.2|Double Jump ability is now activated by pressing Jump midair instead of Sneak.|

The Tarantula Talisman is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] that makes it so every 10th melee hit on the same enemy deals +10% damage.
## Obtaining
The Tarantula Talisman can be obtained as a [[Drop Chance|9Pray RNGesus]] drop from any [[Tarantula Broodfather]] of Tier III or higher after reaching [[Spider Slayer|Spider Slayer LVL VI (6)]].
### Drop Chances
- Tier III: 0.08% (1x)
- Tier IV: 0.17% (1x)
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Tarantula Ring]]|Spider Slayer VII (7) ([[Spider Slayer]])|[[Tarantula Talisman]] 1x Tarantula Talisman 1x [[Digested Mosquito]] 1x [[Tarantula Catalyst]] 64x [[Soul String]] 64x [[Tarantula Silk]]|Recipe: [[Tarantula Silk]] x16 ×4 + [[Tarantula Catalyst]] + [[Soul String]] x32 ×2 + Tarantula Talisman + [[Digested Mosquito]] → [[Tarantula Ring]]|
### Salvaging
Tarantula Talisman can be salvaged for 50 [[Spider Essence]].
## Trivia
- The head's texture is based on a [[Spider Eye]].
- A spider sound plays when the talisman's effect activates.

Ted's Contact Baked In Bread is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] item.
## Obtaining
Ted's Contact Baked In Bread can be purchased from [[Feast Baker Scott]] for 500 [[Kernels]].
## Usage
Ted's Contact Baked In Bread can be consumed to gain [[Feast Chef Ted]] as an [[Abiphones|Abiphone Contact]]. Players with [[Feast Chef Ted]]'s contact can call him to find out which in-season crop will drop [[Seasoning]] 10% more often. This buff affects everyone, even if the player doesn't have the contact.
